Experimental results demonstrate that ARIA achieves strong performance in both 0243 prediction and tone-consistent lyric generation, validating the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [106] [Pseudowords as probes: Large Language Models show little of the sublexical sensitivity that governs human pseudoword processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07936)
*Jing Chen,Giulia Loca,Simona Amenta,Marco Marelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了五个LLM在意大利语假词实验中的表现，发现LLM在有真实词选项时更接近人类行为，但在仅假词条件下表现显著低于字符-n元模型。人类和LLM对子词余弦相似度线索的响应不同，表明LLM可能不具备与人类相同的子词线索敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大型语言模型（LLM）在假词处理中的表现，特别是在涉及子词线索时，是否与人类行为一致。

Method: 通过两个意大利语的两选项强迫选择假词实验测试五种LLM，并将它们的反应与人类行为基准进行对比。

Result: LLM在提供词义线索的真实词选项条件下更接近人类行为，但在只有假词的条件下表现低于字符-n元模型fastText。子词余弦相似度线索未能一致地促进LLM与人类的匹配。人类和快照中在利用子词线索进行推理上的耗费没有一致相关。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，LLM可能不具备人类处理假词时对子词线索的敏感性，进一步讨论了可能的原因包括分词处理和训练数据覆盖率。

Abstract: Systematicity, the probabilistic mapping of form to meaning, permeates language at all levels, and sublexical cues have been shown to govern human pseudoword processing. Yet whether LLMs exhibit comparable sensitivity to these cues remains unclear. We tested five LLMs on two Italian two-alternative forced-choice pseudoword experiments and compared their responses with a human behavioural baseline. LLMs aligned more reliably with humans when real-word options provided a lexical familiarity cue than in the pseudoword-only condition, where they fell substantially below fastText, a character-n-gram model. In addition, the sublexical cosine-similarity cue that reliably drove human--fastText agreement did not consistently transfer to human--LLM alignment, and reasoning-token expenditure bore no consistent relation to human processing difficulty. These findings suggest that LLMs do not necessarily share the sublexical cues that govern human pseudoword processing; we discuss tokenization and training-data coverage as candidate explanations.

</details>


### [107] [Leveraging a four-quadrant approach for evaluating Redpine Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07937)
*Filip Dorm,Leonora Vesterbacka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Redpine Science 提供了一种方法，使模型可以通过 MCP 和 API 访问广泛的文献，相比无文献检索的情况，加入 Redpine Science 的代理正确回答问题的比例提高了，并且在专家验证的问题集中，以 Redpine Science 搜索文献与仅使用网络搜索的代理相比，回答正确率更高。此外，在公开的检索基准测试中，Redpine Science 在召回率方面也有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估 Redpine Science 在提高模型答案质量方面的效果，采用了公开基准和专家验证问题集进行对比实验，以验证其在无偏见和有效性的两方面表现。

Method: 使用 ScholarQABench 科学事实、专家验证问题集和公开检索基准进行三组实验，默认情况和使用 Redpine Science 的情况进行了比较。

Result: 实验结果显示，使用 Redpine Science 的代理在 ScholarQABench 科学事实上的回答正确率提高了 6.8%，在专家验证问题集上回答正确率提高了 9.9%。在公开检索基准测试中，Redpine Science 在召回率上表现优于基线。

Conclusion: 研究表明，Redpine Science 对提升基于文献的模型性能起到了显著作用，并提供了增加透明度和可验证性的专家验证问题集，方便其他研究者复现结果。

Abstract: Redpine Science gives models and agents a single access point to a wide range of peer-reviewed literature, queried directly through the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and an API. This report evaluates Redpine Science on two levels: the relevance of the retrieved chunks, and a model's answer when it has access to Redpine Science compared to web search. Both public and expert-validated benchmarks are used. Public benchmarks are a widely accepted way to test model development and are comparable across labs, but risk saturation and memorization. To address this, we complement them with an expert-validated question set. In total, this report presents four evaluations. On ScholarQABench SciFact, the public answer-quality benchmark reported here, an agent with Redpine Science answers 94.4% of claims correctly against 87.6% with no retrieval. On the expert-validated question set, an agent with Redpine Science states 80.1% of the required claims against 70.2% for an agent restricted to web search. On the 668 queries of a public retrieval benchmark whose gold paper Redpine holds, stripped of any model reasoning, Redpine Science places the correct source paper in its top ten results for 83.1% of queries (Recall@10), against 79.3% for the benchmark's creator. A blinded expert relevance panel places Redpine Science's Precision@5 at 75.2% against 39.8% for the PubMed search tool. We release the expert-validated question set and instructions to reproduce every headline result above, at https://github.com/redpine-ai/benchmarks.

</details>


### [108] [Hybrid Latent Attention for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07940)
*Yuhan Chen,Siyuan Zhang,Nan Wang,Feiyang Kang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HLA提出了一种在循环语言模型中使用滑动窗口和紧凑的潜在表示存储较早的token，以减少缓存大小和增加解码速度，同时保持较高的基准准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前循环语言模型通过增加KV缓存来加深模型，但缓存大小的增加限制了并行序列处理能力并减慢了解码速度。因此，文章提出了一种基于滑动窗口和潜在表示的新方法来解决这些问题。

Method: HLA方法利用滑动窗口存储W个最近的token的精确键值，对于较早的token，则存储为紧凑的潜在表示，允许query直接读取而不重构键值。

Result: 在多个参数量不同的Ouro模型上进行实验，结果显示每token缓存大小减少了10.7倍，每个GPU可处理的并发序列数量增加了4.0至8.8倍，解码吞吐量在1K-token上下文时提高了2.5倍，在16K-token上下文时提高了最高7.4倍。此外，经过监督微调后，HLA在数学、知识和推理基准测试中的准确率保持在97%以上，在长上下文检索中达到96-100%，并且在竞赛级别的数学问题上表现与原始微调模型相当。

Conclusion: HLA通过对缓存的有效管理，为循环语言模型提供了更好的解码性能和并行处理能力，同时保持了较高的模型精度。

Abstract: Looped language models apply the same stack of layers T times to each token, which deepens the model without adding parameters but multiplies its key-value (KV) cache by T. The larger cache limits how many sequences a GPU can decode at once and slows each decoding step, which reads the whole cache. We propose Hybrid Latent Attention (HLA), which keeps exact keys and values within a sliding window of W recent tokens and stores each older token as a compact latent that the query of each loop reads directly, without reconstructing keys and values. We uptrain HLA on Ouro looped models (T=4) with 1.4B and 2.6B parameters, keeping the pretrained weights frozen and training only the added parameters to reproduce the original attention. The cache shrinks by 10.7x per token, fitting 4.0-8.8x as many concurrent sequences per GPU, and decoding throughput improves by 2.5x at 1K-token contexts and by up to 7.4x at 16K. HLA retains over 97% of the original accuracy on math, knowledge and reasoning benchmarks, and 96-100% on long-context retrieval up to 16K tokens. After supervised fine-tuning, it performs on par with the fine-tuned original model on competition-level math.

</details>


### [109] [Structured but Silent: Probing Capability Requirements in LLM Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08018)
*Kyojun Choo,Minsoo Song,Yunju Kang,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探索了LLM隐状态能否在生成之前线性解码查询背后的细颗粒度能力要求，并发现这些能力结构在线性解码方面表现良好，但当要求模型用自然语言明确分类时，准确度显著降低，这种差距被定义为'结构化但沉默'。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在探究语言模型在生成之前能否有效地识别用户的细颗粒度能力需求，并探讨这种识别的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 研究者设计了一个名为TACIT的框架，基于源、转换和世界效果三个维度定义了八个结构化的能力类别。通过1600个平衡的训练查询、合成示例和新领域的场景，训练了四种开源语言模型的线性探针，评估了模型在线性解码能力结构方面的表现。

Result: 实验证明，语言模型能够在线性解码能力结构方面表现出高精度。然而，当模型被要求以自然语言明确分类查询时，准确度显著下降。

Conclusion: 研究结果指出，有关外部能力的要求在线性解码能力中是可获取的，但并不是可靠地表达出来，这一点被定义为'结构化但沉默'的现象。

Abstract: Reliable tool use requires more than triggering a mechanism or matching a query to an API description. Before selecting a specific tool, an agent must first infer the capability requirements implied by the user query. In this paper, we investigate whether these query-side capability requirements are linearly decodable from LLM hidden representations prior to generation, and how this hidden-state accessibility compares with explicit verbal classification. We introduce TACIT, a framework that decomposes external requirements along three fundamental axes: Source, Transformation, and World Effect, defining eight structurally distinct capability classes. Using 1,600 balanced training queries from benchmarks, synthetic examples, and new domain scenarios, we train linear probes on pre-generation hidden states from four open-weight LLM families. Our empirical results demonstrate that fine-grained capability structures are linearly decodable with high accuracy across all models. Crucially, however, we expose a representation-to-verbalization gap: these same models are significantly less reliable when asked to explicitly classify the same queries in natural language. This disconnect indicates that information about required external capabilities is linearly accessible in LLM hidden representations but not reliably expressed, a phenomenon we define as "structured but silent."

</details>


### [110] [The Labeling Problem in Hallucination Detection Benchmarks: An Empirical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08026)
*Jorma Valjakka,Juhani Kivimäki,Juha Mylläri,Jukka K. Nurminen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了自动标签策略在评价大型语言模型（LLMs）虚构性时存在的方法学不明确性，发现不同的自动标签策略和标签与人工标注之间存在显著分歧，并且提示源的选择对基准设计至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前自动标记策略在评价LLMs虚构性时存在的方法学不明确性，特别是对参考忠实性和事实正确性标准的应用不一致问题，作者通过实验研究探究了自动标签策略的多样性和与人工标注之间的关系，以及提示源对评价结果的影响。

Method: 作者通过分析三个常用QA数据集和三种生成器模型下900个人工标注的问题-答案对，评估了不同自动标签策略和人工标注的一致性，并在控制提示条件的情况下，比较了基于词相似度度量、基于参考蕴含的NLI基线和七个LLM法官作为标签者的表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，不同的自动标签策略和标签与人工标注之间存在显著分歧，许多策略表现出强大的方向性错误偏见。使用事实正确性提示相比于忠实性提示，增加了与人工标注的一致性并减少了虚假阳性主导。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，自动虚构性标签应被视为基准设计的一个核心部分，其选择应该被明确、验证并匹配基准目标的意图。

Abstract: In recent years, several methods for detecting when large language models (LLMs) hallucinate have been developed. These methods are often benchmarked with open-domain question answering (QA) datasets containing questions and corresponding short reference answers. First, an LLM is used to generate answers to questions within the QA dataset. Then, some automated labeling strategy is used to label these answers as hallucinated or not by comparing them with the reference answers in the dataset. This evaluation setting creates a methodological ambiguity between two criteria: reference faithfulness (whether the answer is fully supported by the reference) and factual correctness (whether the answer is free from contradictions and factually false specific claims). In practice, automated labelers may apply the former criterion even when the intended target is the latter. We study this potential criterion mismatch using 900 human-labeled question-answer pairs spanning three commonly used QA datasets and three generator models, with labels targeting answer-level factual correctness. We evaluate lexical similarity metrics, a reference-entailment NLI baseline, and seven LLM judges under controlled prompt variants as automated labelers. Our experiments reveal substantial disagreement both among automated labeling strategies and between these labels and human annotations. Many strategies also exhibit strong directional error biases, and for most judge-generator pairs, replacing a faithfulness-oriented prompt with a factual-correctness prompt improves agreement with human annotations and reduces false-positive dominance, indicating that automated hallucination labels depend strongly on how the target criterion is specified. Label-source choice should therefore be considered a fundamental part of benchmark design and made explicit, validated, and matched with the benchmark goal.

</details>


### [111] [Are Language Models Script-Aware?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08037)
*David Kletz,Sandra Mitrović,Ljiljana Dolamić,Fabio Rinaldi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了语言模型在多文种语言上的脚本知识，发现无论大小模型都能很好地匹配输入脚本和遵循脚本指令，但大模型表现更好，尤其是处理非常规脚本组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对语言模型生成输出语言的聚焦使其不能充分理解模型的图形符号响应，有必要进一步研究脚本知识。

Method: 通过两项补充实验来评估模型对输入脚本的适应性和遵循特定脚本指令的频率。

Result: 所有模型在拉丁脚本一致性方面表现出色（超过98%），并且在遵循脚本指令方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 尽管模型都展示了脚本知识，但大模型在标准和非常规脚本组合的表现更好。

Abstract: Language models frequently generate outputs in unintended languages or scripts, a phenomenon known as off-target generation. While existing research has focused on language selection, the dimension of script knowledge remains understudied: before any linguistic understanding can occur, users must recognize the graphic symbols in a model's response. We investigate whether Small and Large Language Models (SLMs and LLMs) possess script knowledge by testing them on multi-scriptic languages. Through two complementary experiments, we evaluate whether models (1) adapt their output script to match the input, and (2) follow explicit instructions to generate text in a specified script. The models we tested demonstrate substantial script knowledge: they all achieve a near-perfect Latin script fidelity (more than 98%) and follow script instructions with high frequency. Nevertheless, we notice differences between LLMs and SLMs, with higher scores for LLMs including for non-standard script combinations.

</details>


### [112] [Language Carries the Expert's Impression: Instrument-Anchored LLM Judges Transfer Counseling-Quality Assessment and Beat In-Domain Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08055)
*Tobias Hallmen,Elisabeth André*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过跨领域迁移训练，探索了不同咨询语料库（包含模拟医疗和学校相关场景）中专家整体印象预测的可能性，发现跨领域训练的效果优于同领域训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前在二元咨询对话中的通信质量自动评估方面，专家评定的数据集较小且成本高昂。本研究旨在通过跨领域迁移学习来探索能否利用不同领域中的数据来提高评估模型的效果。

Method: 研究使用了德国三个不同场景的模拟咨询对话数据集（两个一般医疗服务，一个学校相关家长教师会谈），并对这些数据集进行了跨领域迁移训练。研究以专家评级的数据集为基础，采用小规模开放权重的语言模型来生成会话级别的结构分数，并结合不同机器学习模型预测专家的总体印象。

Result: 跨领域迁移训练的效果优于同领域训练。留一领域外的迁移学习 Spearman 相关系数达到了 0.54，而同领域内训练的最大相关系数仅为 0.48。通过跨领域迁移训练的方法，研究人员发现会话层面的结构分数能够显著提升模型预测专家整体印象的准确性，尤其是结合不同的模型家庭和非言语双向信息后，预测效果进一步提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在实际可达到的数据集大小范围内，专家的总体印象主要取决于所说的内容，以及其他咨询程序的数据，而非仅仅依赖于自己领域内的数据。这为提高心理咨询对话质量自动评估模型的效果提供了新的思路和方法。

Abstract: Automatic assessment of communication quality in dyadic counseling conversations is bottlenecked by data: expert-rated corpora are small and expensive to grow. We study cross-domain transfer of expert overall-impression prediction across three German corpora of simulated counseling (two general-practice medical, one school-related parent-teacher; $n=195$ expert-rated sessions, one corpus after scale equating). Training on the other domains beats training in-domain: leave-one-domain-out transfer reaches nested Spearman $ρ= 0.54$ against $\le 0.48$ within the target domain, a paired session-level gap of $+0.15$ that holds at $+0.12$ when the training-set sizes are matched, so it is not simply data volume. The decisive features are session-level construct scores from small open-weight LLMs reading the two-speaker transcript, with the constructs largely derived from the experts' rating instruments: the instrument-derived battery lifts a single judge from $0.32$ to $0.41$ over generic dialogue qualities, judges from three model families ensemble to $0.51$ language-only, and a nonverbal-dyadic block adds $+0.03$ more, not separable from noise at this sample size. We also price the recording setup: one corpus lost its per-speaker audio, 16% of its diarised segments carry the wrong speaker, and repair is worth $+0.07$ there. At practically attainable corpus sizes, the expert's overall impression is carried by what is said, and by other communication programs' data more than by one's own.

</details>


### [113] [DirectSpeech2LLM: A Simple End-to-End Framework to Mitigate Prompt Overfitting in Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08085)
*Hemant Yadav,Sunayana Sitaram,Roger Zimmermann,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DirectSpeech2LLM 提出了一种简洁的端到端框架，能够在未见过的任务上保持指令遵循能力，通过基于距离的 CTC 损失和贪婪 CTC 标签来计算语音嵌入，不依赖于显式的回归损失便能进行几何对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自动语音识别（ASR）指令模型对于新任务的泛化能力差的问题，尤其是当这些模型仅在ASR任务上进行了训练时。

Method: DirectSpeech2LLM 计算了一种基于距离的 CTC 损失，处理事先冻结的语言模型嵌入矩阵，并利用贪婪 CTC 标签生成几何和时间对齐的语音嵌入输入语言模型。

Result: 在仅使用 960 小时的 LibriSpeech ASR 数据训练后，DirectSpeech2LLM 不仅在 ASR 任务上表现良好，而且能够零样本学习并能很好的泛化到新的任务（如语音翻译和情绪识别）。

Conclusion: 与之前的方法相比，研究发现几何对齐的强度比之前认为的要小，直接CTC损失为模型提供了足够的隐式几何约束。结果表明，该框架在不同的语言模型家族中具有高度的一致性，并且结果随更多训练数据和模型容量的增加而改善。

Abstract: Speech-LLMs often exhibit prompt overfitting, where models solely trained on automatic speech recognition (ASR) instruction fail to generalize to new instructions such as speech translation and continue to behave primarily as ASR system. We propose DirectSpeech2LLM, a simple end-to-end framework that preserves the instruction-following ability of the LLM on unseen tasks when conditioned on speech. It computes distance-based CTC loss over the frozen LLM embedding matrix and uses greedy CTC labels to derive geometrically and temporally aligned speech embeddings respectively as an input to the LLM. Trained solely on 960 hours of LibriSpeech ASR data, DirectSpeech2LLM outperforms the cascaded system on ASR (seen task) and generalizes zero-shot to speech translation and emotion recognition (two unseen tasks), closely matching the cascaded system upper bound on these two new instructions despite seeing neither during training. We also find that geometric alignment strength plays a smaller role than previously assumed, as our modified CTC loss is shown to provide sufficient implicit geometric grounding without requiring an explicit regression loss. Results are consistent across two LLM families and scale with both more training data and model capacity.

</details>


### [114] [SAGE: Semantic Anchor-Guided Evolution for Grounded Medical QA Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08093)
*Chuan Li,Chengyu Wang,Cen Chen,Ye Lyu,Mingyuan Fan,Ming Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAGE 是一种数据合成框架，通过结合轻量级别的语义锚点和迭代的原子与关联合成方法，能够生成高质量的医学训练数据，特别适用于资源受限的临床环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学问答任务模型受限于高质量专家标注数据的稀缺性。SAGE 提供了一种解决方案，通过减少对外部资源和技术栈的依赖，提升模型训练的数据效率和资源利用率。

Method: SAGE 使用公开可用的小型语义分类（如 MeSH）作为语义锚点，结合迭代的原子（单个概念基础）和关联（关系基础）合成方法生成高质量的训练数据。该过程从少量种子数据开始，通过逐步增强提高数据质量。

Result: SAGE 合成的训练数据在多个医学问答基准测试中表现优于使用自我获取或传统文档驱动方法的模型，展现出明显的数据效率提升和资源节省。

Conclusion: SAGE 为资源有限的医学自然语言处理模型开发提供了一种有效且实用的数据生成方案，强调了数据合成框架在提升医学领域模型性能方面的重要作用。

Abstract: Developing reliable models for clinical tasks, such as Medical Question Answering (QA), is severely constrained by the limited availability of high-quality, expert-annotated training data. This challenge is exacerbated by stringent privacy requirements and the impracticality of utilizing large open-source corpora or proprietary cloud APIs within resource-limited clinical settings. To address these obstacles, we introduce SAGE (\textit{Semantic Anchor-Guided Evolution}), a novel data synthesis framework that enables small, locally deployed models to generate high-quality medical training data. SAGE leverages lightweight, publicly available taxonomies such as MeSH as semantic anchors, imposing a structured prior to effectively guide and ground the data generation process. At its core, SAGE iteratively interleaves atomic (individual concept-based) and associative (relation-based) synthesis, bootstrapping training data from minimal seeds. This approach eliminates the need for large collections of medical documents or reliance on external APIs, providing a practical solution for on-premises data creation. Extensive experiments across multiple medical question-answering benchmarks demonstrate that models fine-tuned with SAGE-synthesized data consistently outperform those trained using self-derived or conventional document-based paradigms, highlighting tangible improvements in data efficiency and resource utilization for medical LLM development. Code is available at https://github.com/DIaacKr/SAGE.

</details>


### [115] [Penalty-Framed No-Valid-Option MCQA: Analyzing LLM Abstention under Invalid Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08153)
*Jinhyeok Kim,Hye-Young Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在提供无效选项集的场景下，语言模型的选择题回答表现。通过数学子集的MMLU-Pro数据集，模型需从剩余选项中选择或输出ABSTAIN，若强制选择错误，则会被惩罚。尽管模型遵循了避免无效选择的指令，但仍存在部分正确回答而强制选择错误的情况。这表明基于惩罚的无效选项选择题回答能够揭示模型可靠性中未被标准答案选择准确性覆盖的方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的选择题回答测评标准主要依据正确答案的选取，但在实际应用中，可能遇到无效选项集，即所有选项都不正确的情景。本研究旨在评估在这种场景下语言模型的表现及其可靠性。

Method: 使用MMLU-Pro的数学子集，移除标注的正确答案，允许模型选择剩余选项或输出ABSTAIN，对强制选择错误的行为进行惩罚。

Result: 尽管模型遵循了无需无效选项选择的指令，但实验结果显示，模型仍能在一些原本正确的回答中产生强制选择错误的情况。这表明即使在明确的无无效选项意识和惩罚机制下，模型的可靠性和准确性也存在局限。

Conclusion: 本研究通过基于惩罚的无无效选项选择题回答方法，揭示了当前模型可靠性与其他标准度量方法之间存在的差异。

Abstract: Multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) is commonly used to evaluate large language models under the assumption that one of the provided options is correct, typically using answer-selection accuracy. However, in real deployments, users or retrieval systems may provide invalid option sets in which none of the listed choices is correct, and selecting one of them may incur downstream cost. We study this setting as penalty-framed no-valid-option MCQA. Using the mathematics subset of MMLU-Pro, we remove the labeled correct option, allow models to either choose a remaining option or output ABSTAIN, and penalize invalid forced-choice responses. We further introduce correct-conditioned analysis, evaluating abstention only on instances that the model originally answered correctly. Experiments show that high MCQA accuracy does not fully guarantee abstention reliability: even under explicit no-valid-option-aware instructions and penalty-based scoring, models still produce invalid forced-choice responses for a subset of originally correct instances. These results show that penalty-framed no-valid-option MCQA reveals an aspect of model reliability not captured by standard answer-selection accuracy.

</details>


### [116] [Making COMET Comparable Across Scripts: Diagnosis and Correction of Tokeniser-Induced Script Bias in Indic MT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08159)
*G. L. John Salvin,Swapnil Hingmire*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究测试了翻译质量评估工具COMET在不同书写系统下的表现，发现不同书写系统的得分存在差异，主要归因于分词器的偏差。通过调整得分分布和公布相关信息，可以提升不同书写系统间评分的可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决在不同书写系统下评估翻译质量时存在的Script Invariance假设不成立的问题，该研究通过重新编码目标文本为拉丁字母，验证并量化了书写系统对COMET评分的影响。

Method: 研究者重新编译了目标文本，将其转化为拉丁字母，同时保持内容和人工评分不变。通过分词器和三种无标签诊断工具来分析该偏差的影响。

Result: 研究发现，书写系统的差异贡献了22.9%的本地书写系统COMET评分的变化，且不同评价者的同意程度在五种语言中均有下降。通过COMET-QN对评分进行调整后，得到了约17.1%的额外敏感度恢复。

Conclusion: 研究报告了书写系统对评分影响的详细分析，并提出使用规范化得分和公开相关信息的方法，以改善不同书写系统间评分的可比性。

Abstract: COMET reports translation quality as a single number, and that number is routinely compared across target languages written in different scripts. Such a comparison assumes Script Invariance: the score should not depend on the writing system that carries the target. We test it on IndicMT Eval by re-encoding the target into Latin script, which changes orthographic form while holding content and human ratings fixed. Script identity then accounts for 22.9% of native-script COMET variance, and agreement with annotators falls in all five languages studied. We trace the effect to the tokeniser and measure it with three label-free diagnostics. The bias is two faults, not one. Scores from different scripts occupy incompatible ranges, and within a single script the metric orders translations less accurately. No order-preserving transform of the score can repair the second fault. The first is removed exactly by COMET-QN, which maps the score distribution of each (language, script) pair onto a shared reference. Pooled agreement with annotators rises from 0.300 to 0.399, which is what makes scores from different scripts safe to place on one axis, and every within-language ordering is provably preserved. A regressor over parity features recovers a further 17.1% of the lost sensitivity. The remainder belongs to the encoder, and no post-processing can reach it. We therefore recommend publishing the normalised score, the three diagnostics, and the identity of the tokeniser they were computed against, so that a reader can tell how much of a score reflects translation quality and how much reflects the writing system.

</details>


### [117] [STRUCTURALCOST: A controlled reading time dataset for modeling human sentence processing difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08208)
*Nina Nusbaumer,Iria de-Dios-Flores,Corentin Bel,Christophe Pallier,Guillaume Wisniewski,Benoît Crabbé*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STRUCTURALCOST 是一个包含 475 名参与者和 40,800 个观察值的数据集，用于研究长距离主谓依赖解析的处理成本。该研究发现模型无法完全模拟人的认知成本，表明模型中存在预测能力与工作记忆成本之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决自然语言处理中对长距离主谓依赖解析的理解不足，利用自我控制阅读数据集来评估语言模型与人类认知过程的匹配度。

Method: 研究团队创建了一个包含大量观察值的自我控制阅读数据集，并使用不同类型的语言模型（包括 n-gram 模型、SVM 和变压器）进行实验，以量化人类和模型在处理不同类型句子时的成本差异。

Result: 实验结果表明，尽管某些模型能够部分模仿人类随着依赖长度增加而延长阅读时间的现象，但它们普遍低估了人类在处理过程中所承受的认知成本。这种差异不仅存在于不同架构之间，而且在整个模型大小范围内都存在。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了现有语言模型在长期句法嵌入问题上的局限性，并为评估和改进语言模型的认知准确性提供了数据基础。

Abstract: We introduce STRUCTURALCOST, a self-paced reading dataset of 475 participants and 40,800 observations isolating the processing cost of long-distance subject-verb dependency resolution. We replicate a low-powered psycholinguistic finding at NLP scale, namely that human reading times at the main verb increase with dependency length, driven by syntactic embedding beyond linear distance. Different language models -- spanning n-gram models, SSMs, and transformers -- partially mirror this graded difficulty profile, yet underestimate the integration cost humans incur, with a gap that persists across architectures and model sizes. This suggests these models capture the predictive component of human processing but not the full integration cost that working memory imposes. STRUCTURALCOST provides data needed to drive progress toward evaluating the cognitive plausibility of language models.

</details>


### [118] [Memory Depth and Reconstructed Context Width: A Controlled Evaluation of Hierarchical Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08300)
*Michael Andreev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了长期对话记忆中结构深度和提供给答案模型的上下文宽度之间的互动，发现增加宽度显著提高准确率，而增加深度则没有单调改进，建议进一步研究大而连贯的上下文块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM系统的复杂性增加，了解长期对话记忆的有效参数设置变得至关重要，以便在对话模型中实现更好的表现。

Method: 使用EverMemBench评估不同程度的深度（D1-D4）和核心预算（1024/2048/4096个token）及生产运行和Oracle条件（多达完整的档案），逐步分析。

Result: 增加上下文宽度（从1K到4K）提高了准确率，10.11-17.98个百分点。深度增加不提供单调收益，性能在大于8-16K时达到平台期，但回答的token数量继续增加。Oracle条件在完整档案（68-71K token）上保持高质量。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在LLM的长期对话记忆参数设置中，宽度比深度更为重要，且研究建议转向研究大而连贯的上下文块而不是逐步加深的记忆结构。

Abstract: Long-term conversational memory is becoming an integral component of modern LLM systems. Proposed architectures group records by topics and events, construct hierarchies and graphs, and connect facts through causal and temporal relations. We experimentally study the interaction between two memory parameters: structural depth and the width of context supplied to the answer model. Using EverMemBench, we evaluate depths D1-D4, core budgets of 1,024/2,048/4,096 tokens, and additional Production and Oracle conditions up to the full archive. Increasing width from 1K to 4K improves Accuracy by 10.11-17.98 percentage points, whereas increasing depth provides no monotonic gain. Beyond 8-16K, Production performance reaches a plateau while tokens per correct answer continue to increase; Oracle preserves quality on full archives of 68-71K tokens. These results motivate further investigation of large, coherent context blocks instead of progressively deeper memory structures.

</details>


### [119] [Language Unalignability: Why Some Concepts Resist Cross-Cultural Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08303)
*Shu-Kai Hsieh,Da-Chen Lian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文挑战了当前多语言大型语言模型评估中隐含的翻译-同构假设（TIA），提出该假设在某些概念类别上无效。作者使用用法云框架量化这种失败，并提供了行为和机制上的证据，最终提出了一个多维度的诊断配置文件以识别不可映射的概念类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为多语言大型语言模型（LLMs）评估中的翻译-同构假设（TIA）在某些语言概念类别上不成立，这会妨碍模型准确理解这些概念，这要求对模型的评估方法进行改进，以更好地理解和尊重文化多样性。

Method: 作者使用用法云框架（representation of concepts as point sets of contextualized embeddings）量化这一失败，并研究了多语言模型之间的行为表现和干预机制的差异。

Result: 研究发现，多语言模型在翻译上出现失败与语言家族和资源类别有关，而不是书写系统。多语言模型在对特定语言的干预上表现不佳，这表明某些语言特征对模型来说是不可操作的。

Conclusion: 作者总结了四个诊断指标以评估多语言模型在特定概念类别的表现，并提出了不应将文化能力简化为单一尺度的观点，强调了识别不可映射类别的必要性。这意在推动一个能够尊重和保留文化差异的多语言人工智能的发展。

Abstract: Current evaluation of multilingual Large Language Models (LLMs) rests on an implicit Translation-Isomorphism Assumption (TIA): that semantic structures across languages are congruent and mutually mappable without loss of information. We argue that this assumption is not merely violated in practice, but ill-posed in principle for a typologically identifiable class of concepts, including pragmatic markers, honorifics, and diachronically stratified terms. We formalize this failure using a usage-cloud framework, representing concepts as point sets of contextualized embeddings. We define $α$-unalignability as the impossibility of any mapping that simultaneously preserves lexical faithfulness (centroid correspondence) and structural faithfulness (local neighborhood topology). We provide three layers of evidence. Behaviorally, we show that FLORES-200 translation failures are predicted by language family and resource class but not by script, and that LOBSTER reasoning scores vary by family. Mechanistically, we report a Representation-Intervention Gap (RIG) in a nine-model case study on Yami: the models' activations encode a regularity along which Yami groups with other low-resource and Austronesian languages, yet interventions on language-specific neurons show no demonstrated advantage over random masks: the regularity is visible but not usable by this intervention. Finally, we operationalize these findings into a multidimensional diagnostic profile: Cycle-Consistency, Pragmatic-Load Disagreement, Manifold-Curvature Mismatch, and RIG. We argue that collapsing cultural competence into a single scalar incentivizes "probabilistic flattening," and that recognizing the unalignable class is a precondition for AI that respects, rather than erases, cultural divergence. This suggests that multilingual alignment is not a single well-defined objective, but a set of mutually incompatible projections.

</details>


### [120] [Foresight-over-Graph: Reasoning Beyond Local Horizons for Knowledge Base Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08388)
*Yang Hong,Yajun Yang,Xin Wang,Liping Jing,Qinghua Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FoG 是一种前瞻性的证据检索框架，通过迭代构建相关证据子图并使用远到近的反馈来引导路径探索，从而在 KBQA 上取得优异表现，同时减少 LLM 的调用次数和 token 使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 引导的图推理方法依赖于局部决策过程，在探索更深层次的图上下文前，可能过早地丢弃了关键路径，导致推理链的难以恢复。因此，提出 FoG 以解决这一局限性。

Method: FoG 直接迭代构造与问题相关的证据子图，并通过远到近的反馈机制引导路径探索。同时，它维护一个紧凑的记忆子图以支持持续探索。

Result: 在广泛使用的 KBQA 度量基准上，FoG 达到了最先进的性能，特别在 CWQ 上取得了 16.58% 的显著提升，同时还减少了 LLM 的调用次数和 token 使用量。

Conclusion: FoG 在 KBQA 中展现出强大的性能和效果，表明它在复杂知识推理中有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in question answering, yet they still frequently suffer from hallucinations on knowledge-intensive tasks. Knowledge graphs (KGs) provide LLMs with structured, interpretable, and updatable factual grounding, making them a promising external knowledge source for reliable reasoning. However, existing LLM-guided graph reasoning methods typically rely on hop-wise greedy or beam-style pruning during evidence retrieval. Such local decision processes are inherently myopic: evidence that appears weak near the source may become crucial only after deeper graph context is explored, causing answer-critical branches to be discarded prematurely and making the reasoning chain difficult to recover. To address this limitation, we propose Foresight-over-Graph (FoG), a foresight-aware evidence retrieval framework for knowledge base question answering (KBQA). FoG iteratively constructs a question-relevant evidence subgraph and uses far-to-near feedback to guide path exploration, and maintains a compact memory subgraph to support continued exploration. Extensive experiments on widely used KBQA benchmarks demonstrate that FoG achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a particularly large improvement of 16.58% in Hit on CWQ, while also reducing LLM calls and token usage. Our code is available at https://github.com/yhong7/FoG .

</details>


### [121] [Knowing When Not to Answer: Cross-Domain and Multi-Turn Generalization of Latent Underspecification Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08413)
*Jerzy Kamiński,Ilya Galyukshev,Artem Kuznetsov,Danil Fedorov,Kirill Redko,Sergey Chuprin,Aidar Shumbalov,Stanislav Chumakov,Anna Kalyuzhnaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过一个带有标记回合状态的多回合基准和评估框架，测试了不同数据集和大型语言模型中不可回答性探针的转移性，发现不可回答性探针在某些数据集上有较好的转移性，但在其他类型上效果不明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员关注大型语言模型在对话过程中如何回答无法从给定信息中得出的问题，以及这种不可回答性信号在多大程度上具有可转移性。

Method: 研究构建了一个带有回合标记的多回合基准，并设计了一个模拟用户的评估框架，使用六种不同的数据集和六个开源的大型语言模型来测试不可回答性的探针。

Result: 探针对具有共享不可回答性背景的数据集（如数学和段落中的缺失信息）具有很好的转移性；对于关于已知未知事件的标记，探针在数学数据集上的表现较差，但在使用词性控制后效果有所改善。单回合探针未能零样本检测对话何时能够被回答；虽然结构化探针能够检测到对话的可回答性，但其表现并不优于简单的词袋分类器。一个基于校准探针的闸门，无需模型微调，能够更准确地检测未指明的回合，但在四个模型中并不总是优于生成或提示整合的基线方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明不可回答探针在某些数据集上表现良好，但对于数学数据集中的某些特定类型效果不佳，现有的技术能够部分检测不可回答性但不总是优于基础模型的方法。

Abstract: Large language models routinely answer questions that cannot be answered from the information given, and in dialogue they answer before enough has been said. Unanswerability is linearly decodable from hidden states, but it is unclear which of its forms share a representation and whether the signal is useful in dialogue. We contribute a turn-labeled multi-turn benchmark (423 conversations, 1,661 labeled turn-states) and an evaluation harness with a simulated user who answers clarifying questions, and use them with six datasets and six open-weight LLMs to test how far probes for unanswerability carry. Probes transfer robustly between datasets that share a ground of unanswerability: missing information in math (AUROC 0.77-0.97) and in a passage (SQuAD 2.0<->MuSiQue, 0.77-0.90). Probes for epistemic "known-unknowns" transfer poorly to math, but this separation weakens under lexical controls and changes with layer and coordinate system, so it remains unresolved. Single-turn probes fail zero-shot to detect when a conversation becomes answerable; in-structure probes recover it, but no better than a bag-of-words classifier. A gate on the calibrated probe, with no model fine-tuning, fires on underspecified turns far more precisely than chance, and its end-task success comes within 0.08 of a gate given the true labels. Yet across four models it does not reliably beat vanilla generation or prompted consolidation. The remaining gap lies mostly in how models use a clarification, not in detection.

</details>


### [122] [Rethinking Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation: From Alignment Coverage to Supervision Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08448)
*Bingxi Hou,Guochao Jiang,Guofeng Quan,Weiqing Li,Wenfeng Feng,Guohua Liu,Yuewei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过在特定位置提供精确监督，提高了基于策略蒸馏（OPD）的学习效果。尽管增加监督覆盖范围会降低准确性，但重点置于可靠监督上，用较小范围的精确监督，效果更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在不同模型（标记器）之间的策略蒸馏（OPD）中，如何改进基于严格对齐位置的监督覆盖，以实现更有效的学习。

Method: 该研究对比了不同学生和教师模型之间的预测，并在序列和词汇层面上对齐。通过限制学生选择共享词汇集合中的前16个元素进行监督，实验了该方法的准确性。此外，还测试了在词汇不匹配组上添加均方误差监督的效果。最后，研究了只针对严格位置的监督和整个共享词汇集监督之间的性能差异。

Result: 当仅使用严格对齐位置的监督时，准确性与使用完整共享词汇集的监督相当，这种限制性监督在准确性上优于现有的跨标记器基线。然而，增加在词汇不匹配组上的均方误差监督并未提高性能，反而有所下降。分析表明，对于只有严格损失监督的检查点，跨度梯度与严格梯度之间缺乏或有负的方向一致性，且跨度梯度相对于严格梯度增长。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，从最大化对齐覆盖转向优先确保监督可靠性更为重要，通过集中精确监督，可以取得更好的效果，而不必追求广泛的监督覆盖。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own generations using teacher feedback. With different tokenizers, comparing teacher and student predictions requires alignment at both sequence and vocabulary levels. In this paper, we examine whether expanding this alignment coverage improves learning. Across three heterogeneous teacher--student pairs on mathematical reasoning and code generation, strict 1:1 groups already cover most student-generated tokens despite substantial vocabulary mismatch. On responses sampled from the students before distillation, the shared vocabulary retains nearly all teacher and student probability mass at strictly aligned positions on average. Restricting reverse KL to a student-selected top-16 subset of the shared vocabulary at each strict position achieves accuracy comparable to full shared-vocabulary OPD, outperforming the evaluated cross-tokenizer baselines. Adding mean squared error supervision on span log-probabilities in mismatch groups gives complete supervision coverage, yet reduces accuracy. At checkpoints from training with only the strict loss, the span gradients show weak or negative directional agreement with the strict gradients and grow in magnitude relative to them. These diagnostics may help explain the accuracy drop from adding span supervision. Our findings motivate a shift from maximizing alignment coverage to prioritizing supervision reliability: compact supervision at strict positions can be more effective than broader coverage that introduces weakly aligned or conflicting training signals.

</details>


### [123] [Agentic AutoRAG: RAG Pipeline Optimization through Reasoning-Driven Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08452)
*Lasse B. Strand,Robert Jakob,Kevin O'Sullivan,Markus Kreft*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Agentic AutoRAG通过诊断检索与生成阶段的错误，并在保持准确性和成本之间的平衡后，能够优化RAG系统的超参数，从而在多轮QA基准测试中达到更高的效果，并在成本敏感的应用场景中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的RAG系统配置优化方法忽视了检索与生成性能的具体原因，Agentic AutoRAG通过引入诊断机制和成本意识来增强RAG系统的超参数优化。

Method: Agentic AutoRAG是一种LLM代理优化器，用于多目标RAG超参数优化，并包含诊断器和提议器。诊断器负责将每个失败问题归因于检索或生成，以了解具体问题；提议器基于知识库中的模型排名和定价信息，通过权衡准确性和成本来选择下一步配置。

Result: 在三个多轮QA基准测试中，Agentic AutoRAG的LLM评判准确率超过了所有基线方法；在实际健康护理语料库的成本感知模式下，该系统实现了77%的考试准确率，成本是强基线的约58%；并且达到了强基线约22%的成本，就获得了71.5%的准确率。

Conclusion: Agentic AutoRAG在RAG系统超参数优化方面表现出显著优势，尤其是在成本敏感的应用场景中。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a widely used approach for grounding large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge. However, configuring a pipeline is an expensive hyperparameter optimization problem over many interacting choices, from chunking and embedding model to reranking and generation. Existing optimizers, from greedy search to Bayesian optimization, reduce each trial to an aggregate score and search without modeling why a configuration performed as it did, even though the retrieved chunks already provide evidence about whether each failure occurred during retrieval or after it. We introduce Agentic AutoRAG, an LLM-agent optimizer for multi-objective RAG hyperparameter optimization with retrieval-versus-generation failure attribution. It proposes configurations scored on a frozen exam from the corpus: after each trial a Diagnoser attributes each failed question to retrieval or generation, and a Proposer, grounded in a knowledge base of model rankings and pricing, selects the next configuration, weighing accuracy against cost to trace a Pareto frontier. On three multi-hop QA benchmarks it reaches higher LLM-judge accuracy than every baseline we compare, and within its first 10 trials it matches or beats the statistical baselines' full 30-trial judge accuracy. In its cost-aware mode on a real-world healthcare corpus it reaches a median exam accuracy of 77%, above the strongest baseline's 71.5%, at about 58% of that baseline's cost per query, and it matches that 71.5% at about 22% of the cost.

</details>


### [124] [UNREAL: Unifying Retrieval and Long-Context with a Single Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08463)
*Edan Kinderman,Elad Hoffer,Yochai Blau,Brian Chmiel,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UNREAL 是一种模型内部的证据选择框架，可以在整个语料库检索和长上下文推理范围内选择证据。实验表明，UNREAL 在多种任务上都优于现有的检索增援生成系统，并且在长上下文任务中也能有效减少干扰，提升生成准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的长上下文推理和检索增强生成(RAG)系统处理证据选择的方式不同，UNREAL 旨在探索一种能够跨越这种范围的单机制模型内部证据选择方法。这是因为现有的方法可能无法充分地利用大型语言模型的强大能力，并且可能存在规模不经济的问题。

Method: UNREAL 通过将片段编码并直接从冻结的 LLM 内部表示中推导检索查询来工作，这样不需要增加大量的可训练参数，并且保留了主干结构。研究者评估了在大规模维基百科索引上的各种稠密和混合版本的 UNREAL，并将其应用于长上下文任务，以减少干扰并改善生成结果。

Result: 实验结果表明，UNREAL 在多种数据集上均优于现有的检索增强系统。具体而言，在 HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA 和 MuSiQue 上的召回率分别提高了 24.1%、28.4% 和 5.6%。对于长上下文任务，UNREAL 的表现同样出色，例如 NoLiMa 在最大上下文长度下的准确性从 1.0% 提高到 24.83%，LV-Eval 的 F1 分数从 49.97% 增加到 54.66%。此外，UNREAL 相对于全上下文推理减少了 FLOPs，并在很大程度上降低了时间消耗。

Conclusion: 通过实验证明，UNREAL 提供了一种有效的证据选择方法，可以在长上下文推理和大规模语料库检索之间建立共同基础。这种方法不仅能够扩大现有模型的适用范围，还可以提高模型的效率并降低计算成本。

Abstract: Long-context inference and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) handle evidence selection at vastly different scales, from a single long prompt to an entire corpus. We ask whether a single model-internal mechanism can select evidence across this range. We introduce UNifying REtrieval And Long-Context with a Single Model (UNREAL), a model-native evidence selection framework to span corpus retrieval and long-context inference. UNREAL encodes chunks and derives retrieval queries directly from the frozen LLM's internal representations. It adds fewer than 500K trainable parameters and leaves the backbone unchanged. On a 3B-token, 21M-chunk Wikipedia index, all four dense and hybrid UNREAL backbones outperform state-of-the-art retriever-reranker systems. The best model raises recall from 49.1% to 73.2% on HotpotQA, from 31.7% to 60.1% on 2WikiMultiHopQA, and from 8.8% to 14.4% on MuSiQue. Applied to long-context tasks, the same selection mechanism removes distractors before generation, raising NoLiMa accuracy from 1.0% to 24.83% at its maximum context length of 128K tokens, and LV-Eval's F1 score from 49.97% to 54.66% at 256K. UNREAL also reduces FLOPs and time-to-first-token relative to full-context inference from roughly 32K tokens onward, with larger gains as context grows. Together, these results establish model-internal evidence selection as a common foundation for corpus retrieval and evidence-sparse long-context inference.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [GAMEGO: Training Game-Dev Agents with Synthetic Trajectories Anchored in Real-World Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06910)
*Haoyue Yang,Jingyao Li,Zhengfan Wu,Jing Liu,Xuanle Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了GameGo框架，通过将简短的游戏种子转化为详细的项目需求文档来生成复杂游戏，同时保留核心游戏机制并促进设计探索。GameGo通过在特定任务中动态压缩信息以提高信息密度，而不会限制设计空间。研究建立了GameGoData和GameGoBench数据集，并在游戏开发基准测试中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大型语言模型在基于浏览器的游戏生成方面显示出了显著的能力，但由于用户查询稀疏，编码代理往往要做出不明确的假设，导致游戏机制不完整、玩法不连贯和视觉效果有限。为了改善这一问题，研究提出了GameGo框架。

Method: GameGo框架通过将简短的游戏种子转变为详细的项目需求文档，并通过特定任务的动态压缩算法提高信息密度，同时保持设计的开放性，来促进游戏的自动化开发。

Result: 研究构建了包含2D、2.5D和3D游戏开发轨迹的GameGoData数据集及涵盖124种不同游戏查询的GameGoBench基准测试集。使用GameGoData训练的GameGoCoder模型在游戏开发基准测试中表现出色，优于基准模型，并与当前先进技术相当。

Conclusion: GameGo提供了一种系统的方法来克服直接从稀疏用户查询生成复杂游戏时遇到的问题，通过详尽的项目需求文档驱动的游戏合成过程，确保了游戏的核心机制得到保留的同时，促进了多样化的设计探索。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in web front-end execution, with browser-based game generation emerging as a particularly prominent frontier. While previous efforts frequently rely on complex multi-turn workflows or focus on static game evaluation benchmarks, this work targets direct end-to-end real-world game synthesis driven by coding agents. However, generating complex games directly from sparse user queries often forces coding agents to make underspecified assumptions, yielding incomplete mechanics, disconnected gameplay flows, and limited visual aesthetics. To resolve this issue, this paper presents GameGo, a scalable framework that systematically transforms brief game seeds into comprehensive Product Requirements Documents grounded in industry game-development practices. To retain core gameplay constraints without restricting design exploration, GameGo uses task-specific dynamic compression to maximize information density while preserving instruction following. Based on this pipeline, GameGoData is constructed with 55,060 development trajectories across 2D, 2.5D, and 3D games, alongside GameGoBench, a benchmark comprising 124 diverse game queries. Training GameGoCoder on GameGoData yields a model that outperforms matched baselines and is comparable to frontier models across gamedev benchmarks. All code, datasets, and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [126] [Text2Dashboard: A Governed Agent Architecture for Natural-Language Dashboard Generation over Enterprise DataBrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06914)
*Yiou Wu,Zezhi Tang,Ningwei Bai,Liuhaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Text2Dashboard 是一个针对DataBrain的原型工具，能够将自然语言分析请求转化为可查看的仪表板。该工具包含插件和独立代理运行时，能够实现模型约束决策、类型化工具、持久状态和确定性Hook等功能。评估结果显示，Text2Dashboard 在元数据和SQL任务上达到了较高的准确度，但在更复杂的任务中表现一般。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高数据分析的效率和准确性，研究人员开发了 Text2Dashboard，旨在通过自然语言处理技术将用户的分析请求直接转化为可查看的仪表板。

Method: 该工具利用了插件和独立代理运行时结构，结合了约束模型决策、类型化工具、持久状态等功能，并通过确定性的Hook来实现对执行过程的控制和记录。具体的评估方法包括模拟真实任务和故障注入。

Result: 初步评估结果显示，Text2Dashboard 在处理元数据和SQL任务上表现良好，但在复杂任务中的表现一般。所有故障场景都达到了预期效果，且在记录和状态转移方面表现得当。

Conclusion: 尽管初步结果显示了Text2Dashboard在数据处理和事务控制上的潜力，但其在面对更复杂任务时的表现仍有待提高，且未证实其在生产环境的适用性和效率上的优势。

Abstract: Text2Dashboard is a DataBrain-specific prototype that turns natural-language analytic requests into inspectable dashboards. An installable Codex plugin and standalone Agent Runtime combine schema-constrained model decisions with typed tools, persistent state, and deterministic Hooks for approval, audit, checkpointing, recovery, and failure handling. The pipeline resolves entities, discovers metadata, enforces read-only SQL, composes dashboards, and applies static checks, dynamic preflight, and browser inspection. The model proposes actions while deterministic software controls execution and records state transitions.
  We evaluate the workflow on frozen real-DataBrain tasks and controlled Hook faults. Strict success was 6/8 on metadata and SQL tasks: metadata selection passed 4/4, all four SQL tasks met semantic criteria, and 2/4 met the exact output-column contract. The final release passed 4/4 single-panel dashboard tasks, one two-panel task, and one existing-dashboard refinement; a parameterised task exceeded its step limit. All ten fault scenarios met their specified outcomes without unapproved external side effects. Model inference accounted for over 97\% of observed runtime in every reported group. These small, DataBrain-specific results do not establish production readiness, general text-to-SQL accuracy, or an efficiency advantage over manual dashboard construction.

</details>


### [127] [FluidPD: In-Place Elasticity for SLO-Aware Prefill-Decode Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06917)
*Kartik Ramesh,Kaidi Fu,Zihan Zheng,Jiahuan Yu,Fabio Oliveira,Carlos Costa,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FluidPD 是一种 P/D 分离的服务系统，通过实时调整需求并利用解码侧的余量来处理短暂的不平衡，并通过在同一岗位之间重新分配工作来应对持续的不平衡，从而提高服务的 SLO 达成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统中的预填充/解码工作负载表现出短时间内的突发性和长时间的动态变化，导致系统配置失衡，增加了响应延迟。为了应对这些问题，作者提出了 FluidPD，用于提高服务的 SLO 达成率。

Method: FluidPD 通过引入两个机制来处理预填充和解码需求之间的暂态不平衡和持续不平衡。FluidToken 通过将部分预填充计算任务转移到解码侧的计算资源中分配，而 FluidRole 则通过在同一服务中切换角色来重新分配资源。

Result: 在生产中的 Azure 追踪工作负载中，与静态配置的服务（SGLang）相比，FluidPD 的 SLO 达成率提高了 94.6 个百分点，验证了 SLO 意识下的 P/D 弹性可以显著地提高服务质量。

Conclusion: 作者通过实验证明，SLO 意识下的 P/D 弹性在不增加额外工人配置的情况下能够显著改善服务的质量。

Abstract: Prefill-decode disaggregation is becoming a common architecture for LLM serving because it separates two phases with distinct execution patterns and SLO objectives. Existing systems typically combine a fixed prefill/decode worker ratio with request routing across workers. However, real-world workloads exhibit both short bursts and sustained shifts in the prefill-to-decode demand ratio. As a result, a configuration that is well provisioned at one time may quickly become mismatched, causing latency SLO violations even when idle capacity exists elsewhere. Existing autoscaling mechanisms can add capacity, but they react slowly, require spare GPUs, and do not directly address short-timescale phase imbalance.
  We present FluidPD, a P/D-disaggregated serving system that provides SLO-aware in-place elasticity. FluidPD introduces two complementary mechanisms. FluidToken handles transient imbalance by offloading a bounded portion of prefill computation to decode workers when decode-side slack is available. FluidRole handles sustained imbalance by reassigning running workers between prefill and decode roles in place, avoiding model reload and engine restart. Both mechanisms are guided by lightweight pressure indices that expose prefill and decode-side resource pressure before they appear as SLO violations. Across production Azure trace workloads, FluidPD improves overall SLO attainment over static SGLang by up to 94.6 percentage points, demonstrating that SLO-aware in-place P/D elasticity improves service quality without provisioning additional workers.

</details>


### [128] [Anchor Divergence for Semantic Geometry in Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06919)
*Akash Kannan,Kiho Park,Victor Veitch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了语义上下文如何决定学习向量表示中的几何结构。通过对比学习、指数族和信息几何的结合，提出了‘锚点发散’方法，以定义具体语境下的语义几何结构。实验表明，此方法能有效且高效地指定上下文相关的语义相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的相似性测量（如余弦相似性）提供的是固定几何结构，而语义相似性本质上是上下文相关的。因此，需要研究如何通过对比学习等手段来定义适应特定语义结构的几何形状。

Method: 该方法利用对比学习、指数族和信息几何之间的相互作用来建立概率分布与表示空间上的Bregman几何之间的对应关系。通过这种方式，定义了一种新的‘锚点发散’方法，可以为固定表示设定上下文相关的语义几何。

Result: 实验表明，使用‘锚点发散’定义的有效且高效的上下文相关语义相似性在检索任务中表现良好。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的方法为理解和建立上下文相关的语义几何提供了新的视角，并在实际应用中显示了其价值。

Abstract: This paper concerns how semantic context determines geometry in learned vector representations. Similarity is typically measured using cosine similarity, which provides a single fixed geometry. Semantic similarity, however, is inherently context dependent: two images may be similar because they depict the same object, share a visual style, or are relevant to the same clinical finding. We show that contrastive representations naturally encompass a family of geometries that can be specialized to particular semantic structure. The key idea is to use an interplay between contrastive learning, exponential families, and information geometry to establish a correspondence between probability distributions over "anchors" and Bregman geometries on the representation space. We use this correspondence to define "Anchor Divergences", a method for specifying context-specific semantic geometries on fixed representations. Under this correspondence, modeling the anchor distribution models the geometry itself. Experiments on retrieval show that anchor divergences provide an effective and efficient way to specify context-specific semantic similarity.

</details>


### [129] [RadOnc-Agent: An LLM-Orchestrated Framework for AI Workflows Across the Radiotherapy Care Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06923)
*Caiwen Jiang,Shuoyang Wei,Songlin Zhao,Junyu Li,Jingyuan Chen,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为RadOnc-Agent的智能框架，可用于实现放射治疗的四个临床阶段，提供了一系列交互式函数。通过大型语言模型控制器解析临床意图，与专业服务交互，并执行复杂的跨阶段流程。评估结果显示，该系统在单功能执行、跨阶段脚本流程和真实患者流程中的表现优秀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射治疗技术存在临床环节分割、软件环境多样及数据模态不兼容的问题，本文旨在提出一种综合协调各环节的技术框架以提高整体治疗效率。

Method: RadOnc-Agent通过大型语言模型控制器解析临床意图，并通过交互式接口与专业服务互动，以协调复杂的跨阶段流程。

Result: 该系统在单功能执行中成功率达98.79%，跨阶段脚本流程完成率达96.50%，真实患者流程完成率达96.67%。去除纵向状态和使用未验证的调度后，跨阶段流程完成率分别降低至84.00%和增加了错误调度的比例。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，一种大型语言模型驱动的架构能够实现多阶段、多环境和技术的信息协调，但其临床效果和潜在益处仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence has advanced individual radiotherapy tasks, yet these capabilities remain separated across clinical stages, software environments and data modalities. This fragmentation contrasts with the longitudinal radiotherapy workflow from treatment decision-making through follow-up. Here we present RadOnc-Agent, an agentic artificial-intelligence framework that formalizes radiotherapy into four clinical phases and provides 26 callable functions through a conversational interface. A large-language-model controller maps clinical intent to schema-constrained calls, preserves patient and workflow context, and routes requests to specialist services. We evaluated system execution using 2,600 single-function requests (7,800 repeat executions), 200 prespecified synthetic cross-stage scenarios spanning four phases (600 executions), and 120 workflow instances from 60 de-identified patient records (360 clean executions) representing decision-to-planning and planning-to-adaptation. RadOnc-Agent selected the intended function in 98.79% of single-function executions, completed 96.50% of scripted cross-stage workflows, and completed 96.67% of real-patient workflow executions. In comparative ablations, removing longitudinal state reduced cross-stage completion from 96.50% to 84.00%, while disabling schema and identity validation increased mismatched backend dispatch from 0% to 95.28% in a replay/test evaluation. These findings establish the technical feasibility of an LLM-orchestrated architecture for coordinating heterogeneous radiotherapy capabilities and information across longitudinal workflows; they do not establish clinical correctness, clinical utility or prospective benefit.

</details>


### [130] [Metonymic Circuits for Abstract Concept Grounding in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06928)
*Jing Ding,Ziqiao Ma,Jiayuan Mao,Joyce Chai,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现 Vision Transformers 通过一种称为元喻的地基机制来处理抽象概念，该机制通过具体的可解释锚点概念（如火）将视觉信号与抽象语义联系起来。实验证明在精心筛选的图标数据集中存在结构化的元喻电路，视觉原语在早期层占据主导地位，而物体类型的锚点则在抽象目标之前更为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于有局限直接指代证据的数据，提出了一种新的理解抽象概念的机制——元喻地基机制。

Method: 通过使用 Transcoders 对 CLIP 和 DINO 视觉编码器进行应用，以恢复与具体概念相关的中间特征，并追踪这些贡献在抽象概念识别电路中的作用。

Result: 在精心筛选的图标数据集中的实验揭示了结构化的元喻电路，其中感知原语占据了早期层的主导地位，而物体类型的锚点则在抽象目标之前更为显著。图像中包含渲染文本的情况，则触发了另一种感知到文本的路径。

Conclusion: 实验进一步表明，元喻中间体在将视觉信号接地为抽象概念时具有功能作用。

Abstract: We study how Vision Transformers ground abstract concepts (e.g., angry) when training data provide limited direct referential evidence. We hypothesize a metonymic grounding mechanism in which abstract predictions are driven by concrete, interpretable anchor concepts (e.g., fire) that bridge visual signals to abstract semantics. By applying Transcoders on CLIP and DINO vision encoders, we recover intermediate features that can be associated with semantic labels for more concrete concepts, and trace their contributions in circuits underlying abstract concept recognition. Experiments on a carefully curated icon dataset reveal structured metonymic circuits, in which perceptual primitives dominate early layers and object-like anchors precede abstract targets. Images containing rendered text instead recruit a distinct perceptual-to-textual route. Causal interventions further validate that metonymic intermediates are functionally involved in grounding abstract concepts.

</details>


### [131] [Principles that Guide, Actions that Inform: Agent Evolution via Knowledge Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06964)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Junkai Ma,Xiang Yin,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SAGA的新框架，目标是使大规模语言模型代理能够通过经验驱动的知识抽象和利用自我进化。SAGA通过将交互轨迹逐步转化为可重用的原则，并结合纠正性反馈和重采样，实现了执行与抽象间的反馈循环，从而改进了任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的方法主要集中在经验的表示和组织方面，尽管这些方法使代理能够在不修改模型参数的情况下积累经验，但获得的知识仍然紧密耦合于特定任务和上下文中，限制了其一般性。

Method: SAGA框架包括四个阶段：将交互轨迹转化为具有明确适用条件的可重复用的原则描述，通过执行证据保持联系，将提取的原则实例化为特定任务指南，并通过纠正性反馈和重采样优化候选动作。

Result: 在ScienceWorld和ALFWorld实验中，SAGA框架显示出改善了任务性能，且消融研究强调了上下文实例化和动作调节对于利用原则级知识的重要性。

Conclusion: SAGA框架提供了一种新的方法来解决大规模语言模型代理难以不断从经验中进化的问题，通过自我进化的方式提高了这些代理的适应能力和泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in interactive environments, yet their ability to continually evolve from experience remains limited. Although fine-tuning enables adaptation, its dependence on parameter access and high computational costs restrict its flexibility, especially for large-scale and closed-source LLMs. External memory offers an alternative by allowing agents to accumulate experience without modifying model parameters. However, existing methods mainly focus on experience representation and organization, while the acquired knowledge remains tightly coupled with specific tasks and contexts, limiting generalization. A key challenge is how to transform concrete interactions into abstract and reusable knowledge that guides future decisions beyond individual experiences.
  To address this challenge, we propose SAGA (\underline{\textbf{S}}elf-evolving \underline{\textbf{A}}gents through Experience-\underline{\textbf{G}}rounded \underline{\textbf{A}}bstraction), a framework for experience-grounded knowledge abstraction and utilization in LLM agents. SAGA progressively transforms interaction trajectories into episodic descriptions, reusable procedures, and principles with explicit applicability conditions, while maintaining links to execution evidence. Retrieved principles are instantiated into task-specific guidance and used to refine candidate actions through corrective feedback and resampling. This creates an execution--abstraction feedback loop, where accumulated knowledge guides future interactions and new experiences continuously update hierarchical memory. Experiments on ScienceWorld and ALFWorld demonstrate improved task performance, with ablation studies highlighting the importance of contextual instantiation and action regulation for leveraging principle-level knowledge.

</details>


### [132] [AegisFlow: A Multi-Agent Agentic AI Framework for Autonomous Remediation and Self-Healing in Fragile Data Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06971)
*Muhammad Bilal Awan,Zubair Hussain,Abdul Shahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AegisFlow 提出了一个自愈框架，通过使用大型语言模型自动创建、测试和部署代码补丁，显著减少了修复时间，并成功应对了常见的数据流问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的数据管道经常由于上游架构变化等原因失效，观察工具只能发出警报但无法缓解，导致修复时间长、运营疲劳。

Method: 该框架采用Watchdog和Repair代理，通过生成和验证数字孪生环境中的补丁来解决这些问题。具体来说，它使用平行影子补丁执行模式和基于MAPE-K循环的方法。

Result: 实验测试表明，AegisFlow 在 98.1% 的场景下将平均修复时间缩短至 3.2 分钟，并成功修复了 92% 的缺陷。特别地，JSON模式变化的修复成功率达到96%，标点符号漂移的修复成功率为98%，但在Shadow DOM情况下略低，为85%。

Conclusion: AegisFlow显著减少了数据管道故障的修复时间，并有效分配了数据工程师的资源。它具有高度的可部署性，可以以插件形式部署到现有的管道编排系统中，且对现有系统的影响较小。

Abstract: Traditional data pipelines are notoriously brittle, often failing due to upstream schema drift, API contract changes, or website DOM modifications. Present observability tools only raise alerts but for human engineers, resulting in a high Mean Time to Repair (MTTR) and operational fatigue. In this paper we propose AegisFlow (Agentic Engine for Intelligent Self-healing and Graph-driven Operations for Workload remediation), a novel agentic framework that closes the loop between detection and resolution. AegisFlow uses a Watchdog agent to collect runtime telemetry and has a Repair agent to automatically create, test and deploy code patches based on Large Language Models (LLMs). The framework presents the non-intrusive execution model called Parallel Shadow Patching, a non-intrusive execution model based on the Monitor, Analyze, Plan, Execute, Knowledge (MAPE-K) loop to generate and verify patches in digital twin environments. Through experimental testing, we have evaluated AegisFlow across five common failure scenarios, and see 98.1 percent improvement in MTTR (from an average of 170 minutes per patch to 3.2 minutes) and a patch success rate of 92 percent . In particular, the system is successful in dealing with changes in the JSON schema (96 percent ) and punctuation drift (98 percent ), and is least successful in Shadow DOM cases (85 percent ). AegisFlow frees up about 98 percent of data engineering on-call time from firefighting and reallocates it towards innovation. The framework is deployment agnostic consisting of a system that can be deployed in a plugin fashion into an existing pipeline orchestration system with minimal uplift to the existing system.

</details>


### [133] [EPOCH: Reliable Discovery through Evidence-Governed Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06986)
*Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Ruitong Li,Xinle Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EPOCH 通过证据治理的设计，显著提高了在 AlgoTune 和 Math14 测试集上的表现，特别是在算法优化和数学证明方面取得了实质性进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统在优化评估反馈方面表现良好，但在如何有效解析、挑战和重用这些反馈方面存在不足。这导致了具有潜力但不够稳健的候选方案被误认为是发现，而基准改进、有限证明和定理级断言则被过度混杂。

Method: EPOCH 采用了一种证据治理架构，结合了明示任务合同、类型化内存、活跃反驳、准入检查和独立重播等机制，使每个候选方案都能根据它所支持的声明的强度和范围进行评估。

Result: EPOCH 在 AlgoTune 上达到了最先进的综合性能，平均标准化分数为 0.65，超过了最强基准的 0.53，并在内部 Math14 测试套件上达到了最高的平均分数 0.57。此外，在官方测试重播中表现良好，领先于 AgentHPO 的描述性综合。
在十个发现问题中，EPOCH 在可执行构造、优化算法、反例以及证明支持的结果方面取得了显著的特定任务进展。

Conclusion: EPOCH 的结果表明，证据治理对于生成更强大且值得信赖的科学发现的 AI 研究代理是必要的步骤。

Abstract: AI research agents are increasingly used to search over programs, mathematical constructions, and proofs. However, existing systems typically optimize evaluator feedback without adequately governing how that feedback is interpreted, challenged, and reused. As a result, promising but fragile candidates can be promoted as discoveries, while benchmark improvements, finite certificates, and theorem-level claims are too easily conflated. We introduce EPOCH, an evidence-governed architecture designed to close this gap. EPOCH implements an evidence-governed discovery loop by combining explicit task contracts, typed memory, active falsification, admission checks, and independent replay, so that each candidate is evaluated against the strength and scope of the claim it supports. EPOCH achieves state-of-the-art aggregate performance on AlgoTune, substantially exceeding the strongest baseline in mean normalized score (0.65 vs. 0.53), and attains the highest mean score on the internal Math14 suite (0.57). It further shows favorable held-out behavior under official-test replay and leads the descriptive aggregate on AgentHPO. Across ten discovery problems, EPOCH delivers substantial task-specific advances, including improved executable constructions, optimized algorithms, counterexamples, and proof-supported results. These advances demonstrate its ability to convert search into concrete progress across mathematical and computational domains. Together, the results suggest that evidence governance is a necessary step toward AI research agents that produce not only stronger solutions, but also more trustworthy scientific discoveries.

</details>


### [134] [When better traffic forecasts fail to improve signal control: a layered diagnostic study of forecast-to-decision value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06992)
*Jianing Long,Xiaobin Li,Wuming Lei,Weiguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过多层次诊断研究，发现改进的交通预测未必带来更好的信号控制决策，强调了可观察性、行动识别性、动态一致性及目标对齐的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的交通预测改进对信号控制决策的实际效果有限，本研究旨在探索交通流量预测在信号控制优化中的实际价值。

Method: 研究人员评估了点预测、可信区间、依赖预测及闭环控制策略。通过分析重建的中国宣城31天的交通数据，采用多种方法评估优化前后预测的表现。

Result: 结果表明，入口级和动作级预测分别使均方绝对误差减少了4.03%和3.92%。90%的可信区间在整体上实现90.72%的边际覆盖率，但在高需求子集上覆盖率较低。修正时间接口后，因果预测和事件Oracle的表现仍低于预期，甚至在测试日期内增加了排队车辆秒数。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，未来的预测改进在现实应用中须基于实际的可观察性、行动识别性、动态一致性和目标对齐来确保在信号控制中的实际效益。

Abstract: Improved traffic forecasts do not necessarily yield better signal-control decisions. We investigate this gap through a layered diagnostic study using 29 days of reconstructed demand from Xuancheng, China, with seven dates reserved for testing. The framework evaluates point forecasts, conformal intervals, dependence-aware scenarios, and matched closed-loop controllers. Entry-level and movement-level forecasts reduce mean absolute error by 4.03% and 3.92%, respectively, relative to historical means. A nominal 90% conformal interval achieves 90.72% marginal coverage but only 75.66% on an ex-post high-demand subset. Interface audits identify decision-time leakage and reveal that only two of nine controlled intersections offer multiple effective actions. We correct the temporal interface and compare causal forecasts with a five-second event oracle using exhaustive joint-action search. A synthetic positive control demonstrates that future information can reduce the internal rollout cost by 61.5%. On the frozen test dates, however, causal forecasts and the event oracle increase queue vehicle?seconds by 6.09% and 3.39% relative to the matched no-future rollout, while the oracle reduces spillback exposure by 3.78%; paired-day bootstrap intervals cross zero. These findings indicate that forecast value depends on temporal observability, action identifiability, dynamics consistency, and objective alignment. The proposed protocol provides a practical way to diagnose where predictive improvements fail to translate into operational benefits.

</details>


### [135] [Joint upper-bound coverage and route-choice utility: an empirical evaluation on two urban proxy tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06995)
*Jianing Long,Xiaobin Li,Wuming Lei,Weiguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过固定的协议评估了交通预测的准确性与不确定性覆盖对路线选择的影响。结果显示，虽然联合覆盖度有所提高，但同时导致了更多潜在的延误和旅行时间增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过比较不同路径选择下的覆盖度和误差，研究旨在明确更准确的交通预测或更高的不确定性覆盖对路线决策是否具有实际改进作用。

Method: 使用北京和成都的路面数据，构建了包含150个起终点对的线下代理任务，包括三种候选路径和14个测试日，并进行了覆盖率和误差的联合校准。

Result: 研究结果显示，虽然覆盖度得到了提升，但联合校准的上限导致了更高的潜在延误（分别增加了0.1633%、0.7848%和0.9200%）以及平均旅行时间的增加（分别增加了0.588、3.082和4.418秒）。

Conclusion: 联合覆盖度的提高并非下游路线选择的替代指标，在这些固定任务下的离线结果没有确立在线或因果上的好处。

Abstract: Whether more accurate traffic forecasts or higher uncertainty coverage improve route decisions is unclear. We evaluate this question with a frozen protocol that separates speed error, joint candidate path upper bound coverage, route selection, and realized loss. Using processed road speed data from Beijing and Chengdu, we construct offline proxy tasks with 150 origin destination pairs, three candidate paths, and 14 test days per city. We compare raw 90th percentile path time bounds with jointly calibrated upper bounds under minimum bound route choice. Joint coverage rises from 83.19% to 92.26% in Beijing M1, from 75.14% to 88.33% in Chengdu M1, and from 74.01% to 90.64% in Chengdu M2. Yet C2 increases lateness by 0.1633, 0.7848, and 0.9200 percentage points, respectively, and mean travel time by 0.588, 3.082, and 4.418 seconds. In a separate Chengdu predictor comparison, a 14.91% reduction in speed mean absolute error accompanies a 1.4571 percentage point reduction in lateness under C0. Joint coverage is therefore not a surrogate for downstream route utility in these frozen tasks the offline results do not establish online or causal benefits.

</details>


### [136] [Topology-Consistent Task Planning over Cellular Workflow Complexes for LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07004)
*Sen Zhao,Jia Tang,Ruiqi Kong,Zuyu Zhang,Lifeng Shen,Ding Zou,Xinyu He,Xu Zhang,Junwei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoPlanner 是一种拓扑一致的规划框架，将工具依赖图提升为细胞工作流复形，用于 LLM 工具规划。它通过 cosheaf 一致的细胞检索获取相关子复形，进行多维结构推理，并将结果的细胞表示与规划 LLM 接口，生成工具序列。实验证明，该方法在不同的当地 LLM 基准上比基于提示和图增强的基线模型具有一致的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的规划者难以处理包含循环验证、分支合并和可重用中间状态等复杂子任务依赖的工作流模式。为了解决这些问题，提出了 TopoPlanner 作为一种拓扑一致的规划框架。

Method: TopoPlanner 提取与请求相关的闭包子复形，通过 cosheaf 一致的细胞检索，进行多维结构推理，并将细胞表示与规划 LLM 接口以生成工具序列。

Result: TopoPlanner 在四个具有拓扑引导循环、合并和循环合并工作流的工具规划基准上，相比基于提示和图增强的基线模型，显示了一致的性能改进。

Conclusion: TopoPlanner 通过将复杂工具依赖关系编码为拓扑复形，提高了 LLM 在执行涉及多种依赖关系的工作流规划中的表现。

Abstract: Task planning for LLM agents requires workflows that satisfy both user intent and complex sub-task dependencies. While existing planners work well for sequential or directed acyclic graph (DAG)-like structures, they struggle with workflow patterns such as verification-correction loops, convergent branch merging, and reusable intermediate states that arise naturally in real-world tool orchestration. We present TopoPlanner, a topology-consistent planning framework that lifts tool dependency graphs into cellular workflow complexes and uses them as topologyaware context for LLM tool planning. TopoPlanner retrieves a request-relevant closed subcomplex through cosheaf-consistent cellular retrieval, performs multidimensional structural reasoning over the retrieved topology, and interfaces the resulting cellular representation with the planner LLM for tool-sequence generation. Experiments on four tool-planning benchmarks with topology-guided loop, merge, and loop-merge workflows show consistent improvements over prompt-based and graph-enhanced baselines across different local LLM backbones.

</details>


### [137] [When to Rethink: Learning Multi-Perspective Self-Verification for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07018)
*Ziquan Zhu,Hanruo Zhu,Si-Yuan Lu,Morris Yu-Chao Huang,Yicheng Lin,Wei Han,Tianlong Chen,Mingyuan Wu,Hanchao Yu,Gaojie Jin,Lu Liu,Bo Sun,Tianjin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOTIVE框架是多视角自我验证的新方法，通过多种验证视角评估每个候选答案，并通过正确性校准的多视角验证学习得到一致性可靠性评分。在推理过程中，该评分指导接受或重新思考的决策，从而直接返回可靠的答案，并在必要时触发基于历史的重新思考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自我验证方法通常依赖单一的验证标准或固定的提示，导致可靠性估计不完整且不稳，而MOTIVE能够在多任务场景下调用不同见解，提高答案的可靠性。

Method: MOTIVE框架采用多视角验证方式，结合正确性校准理念，对每个候选答案进行多维度评估，通过学习机制获取一致性可靠性评分，以此作为推理决策的依据。

Result: 与强大的自我验证和自我纠错基线相比，MOTIVE在多个跨模态基准和不同VLM基底上表现出稳定且显著的性能提升，特别是在提高接受或重思决策的可靠性以及减少不必要的推理循环方面。

Conclusion: MOTIVE提供了一种不需要外部裁判的可靠跨模态推理方法，它通过多视角验证和一致性可靠性评分帮助改善自我验证过程，提高答案的准确性与效率。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance in multimodal reasoning, yet they remain prone to generating plausible but incorrect answers. Self-verification offers a practical way to improve answer reliability without relying on external judges, but existing methods typically depend on a single verification criterion or fixed prompt, resulting in incomplete and unstable reliability estimates. We first systematically analyze how verifier capability and prompt design affect verification performance. Our findings show that stronger verifiers provide more reliable judgments, while verification performance is highly sensitive to prompt choice, with no single prompt consistently dominating across tasks. Guided by these findings, we propose \texttt{MOTIVE}, a \textbf{M}ulti-View Self-Verificati\textbf{O}n wi\textbf{T}h Rel\textbf{I}ability-Guided Selecti\textbf{VE} Rethinking framework for reliable multimodal reasoning. \texttt{MOTIVE} evaluates each candidate answer from complementary verification perspectives and learns a correctness-aligned reliability score through correctness-grounded multi-view verification learning. During inference, this score governs an accept-or-rethink decision, allowing reliable answers to be returned directly while uncertain ones trigger history-guided rethinking. Extensive experiments across diverse multimodal benchmarks and VLM backbones demonstrate that \texttt{MOTIVE} consistently outperforms strong self-verification and self-correction baselines. Further results show that reliable verification improves accept-or-rethink decisions and reduces unnecessary reasoning turns, enabling more reliable and efficient self-verification without an external judge.

</details>


### [138] [Beyond Refusal Patterns: Safe-Role Internalization for Robust and Generalizable LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07023)
*Jinghao Pang,Jitai Hao,Qiang Huang,Zhaochun Ren,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SSRFT (Supervised Safe-Role Fine-Tuning) 是一种安全对齐框架，通过构建安全角色问题-答案数据集并让模型内化安全原则而非仅通过拒绝模式来应对 jailbreak 攻击，从而提升安全对齐的鲁棒性和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全对齐方法如监督微调（SFT）和基于人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）需要大量特定攻击的监督和计算资源，且在深度安全对齐和过度拒绝方面存在局限。SSRFT 致力于解决这些问题。

Method: SSRFT 通过从心理测量题、有限的 jailbreak 提示和安全角色描述中构建安全角色问题-答案数据集，合成、验证并扩展出多种场景下的角色一致回答，使模型内化安全导向的价值观和原则。

Result: SSRFT 在多个基础和指令模型上展示了比标准 SFT 更强的鲁棒性和泛化性。其对预填攻击更具有鲁棒性，并能更好地向未见过的 jailbreak 领域泛化，同时减少了对良性查询的过度拒绝，并保持了模型的一般能力。

Conclusion: SSRFT 成功地将安全角色内化作为一种有效的替代拒绝中心的安全对齐策略，提升了语言模型在面对 jailbreak 攻击时的安全性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable capabilities but remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that elicit harmful or unsafe outputs. Existing safety alignment approaches, including Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), often require substantial attack-specific supervision and computational resources, while remaining susceptible to shallow safety alignment and over-refusal. To address these challenges, we introduce SSRFT(Supervised Safe-Role Fine-Tuning), the first framework that reformulates safety alignment as the internalization of a predefined safe role. SSRFT constructs a Safe-Role Question-Answer (SRQA) dataset from psychometric questions, limited jailbreak prompts, and a safe-role description. Role-consistent responses are synthesized, validated, and expanded into diverse scenarios, enabling models to internalize safety-oriented values and principles rather than explicit refusal patterns. Experiments across multiple Base and Instruct models show that SSRFT achieves more robust and generalizable safety alignment than standard SFT. SSRFT shows substantially greater robustness to prefilling attacks and better generalization to unseen jailbreak domains, while reducing over-refusal on benign queries and preserving the model's general capabilities. These results establish safe-role internalization as an effective alternative to refusal-centric safety alignment. Warning: This paper contains examples of harmful and toxic language.

</details>


### [139] [Offline AI Modules: Voice-First Offline Architecture, Hardware Reference Stack, Quantization and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07026)
*Sunday Afariogun,Odunolaoluwa Jenrola,Zeinab Nezami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究介绍了离线AI模块的工作流，针对非洲语言社区，提供了模块化的离线语音AI架构、低成本硬件参考物料清单以及面向指令调优语言模型的可复制量化和基准测试管道。研究结果表明，Q4_K_M 量化对两个硬件级别（Jetson Orin NX 和 Raspberry Pi5）的部署具有最佳的大小与质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在满足非洲语言社区的需求，为缺乏互联网连接的地区提供实用、低功耗且社区友好的离线语音AI系统。

Method: 研究设计了包含模块化离线语音AI架构、低成本硬件参考物料清单以及面向指令调优语言模型的可复制量化和基准测试管道的系统。通过评估不同量化格式的三个指令调优模型在两个硬件级别上的部署指标（解码吞吐量、聊天延迟、内存、功耗）和多语言质量（主题分类精度、单语言困惑度偏移）来得出结论。

Result: 评估结果表明，Q4_K_M 量化格式在两个硬件平台上均取得了最佳的大小与质量权衡。gemma-4-E2B-it 在 Jetson Orin NX 型号上实现了 28.8t/s 的解码吞吐量，并且主题分类精度为 89.2%。三个模型均在 Raspberry Pi5 上的 16GB 内存预算内运行。

Conclusion: 研究证明了 Q4_K_M 量化在 Jetson Orin NX 和 Raspberry Pi5 硬件平台上的有效性，这些平台分别代表了较高和较低的技术要求。

Abstract: The Offline AI Modules workstream enables practical, low-power, and community-accessible deployment of voice-first AI systems that operate fully offline. Designed for African language communities where speech is the dominant mode of interaction and internet connectivity is unreliable or absent, the workstream delivers three reinforcing components: a modular voice-first offline architecture, a low-cost hardware reference bill of materials, and a reproducible quantization and a reproducible quantization and benchmarking pipeline for instruction-tuned language models in the 2-5B parameter class. This paper presents the first end-to-end benchmark evaluation of the stack across two hardware tiers: an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX (TierB) and a Raspberry Pi5 (TierA). Three instruction-tuned models are evaluated across four quantization formats, assessed for deployment metrics (decode throughput, chat latency, memory, power) and multilingual quality (topic classification accuracy on MasakhaNEWS across English, Hausa, Igbo, Nigerian Pidgin, and Yoruba; per-language perplexity drift). Speech recognition is evaluated using Ethio-ASR on Amharic and Oromo across both tiers. The principal finding is that Q4_K_M quantization represents the best size-to-quality trade-off for deployment on both tiers: gemma-4-E2B-it achieves 28.8t/s decode throughput and 89.2% topic classification accuracy at Q4_K_M on TierB, while all three models run within the 16GB memory budget on TierA.

</details>


### [140] [JIVEAdapter: A Multi-Task Additive Low-Rank Adapter via Joint and Individual Variation Explained (JIVE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07036)
*Sara Abdali,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JIVEAdapter是一种多任务“加法”低秩适配器，它通过将每个权重更新分解为共同结构和特定任务结构，并优化这些结构之间的关系来提高参数效率。JIVEAdapter在GLUE和SuperGLUE数据集上与单任务和多任务低秩基线相比表现相当，并且在有相关任务时，可以利用已学的共同结构以低成本支持新的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的低秩适配器大多为单任务且代表权重更新的方式单一，忽略了不同任务间的信息共享和特定性。JIVEAdapter设计旨在解决这一问题，引入了基于统计学的JIVE方法，能够区分任务间的共同特性和任务特异性。

Method: JIVEAdapter通过将权重更新分解为共同结构（Joint）和特定任务结构（Individual）来工作。共同结构被优化为接近正交于特定任务结构。共同结构在一个任务组上学习或增量学习，并且在不需要重训练共同部分的情况下，可以用于新任务。

Result: 在GLUE和SuperGLUE任务上，JIVEAdapter的表现与现有的单任务和多任务低秩方法相当。它在拥有相关任务时可以重用共同结构，通过低成本的垂直方向比例调整支持新任务，否则则需训练少量新增的结构。

Conclusion: JIVEAdapter能够在参数高效模型的适应性调整中提供良好的性能，特别是在利用已有相关任务的共同学习成果方面表现出色。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts pretrained models at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, yet most low-rank adapters are single-task and represent each weight update multiplicatively, leaving no explicit account of what is shared across tasks and what is task-specific. We introduce JIVEAdapter, a multi-task "additive" low-rank adapter inspired by statistical Joint and Individual Variation Explained (JIVE). JIVEAdapter decomposes every weight update into a Joint structure shared across all tasks plus a per-task Individual structure, penalizes the Individual structures to be near-orthogonal to the Joint so shared and task-specific signal stay "interpretable" and separated, and allocates rank adaptively across a shared Joint pool and a per-task Individual pool. The Joint is learned once, jointly over a task group or incrementally, one task at a time, then frozen and reused as a prior for new tasks without retraining the shared part. On GLUE and SuperGLUE with DeBERTaV3-base, JIVEAdapter is competitive with strong single-task and multi-task low-rank baselines at a matched per-task effective rank, without extra modules such as MoE, and when a related held-in task exists its frozen Joint serves a held-out task by reusing that task's Individual with only a cheap per-direction scale, otherwise training a small new one.

</details>


### [141] [Inference-Time Projection for Physically Valid Biomolecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07037)
*Qurat-ul-ain,Yee Whye Teh,Charlotte M. Deane,Matteo Cagiada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种方法，通过局部、稀疏和位移限制的投影操作来解决AlphaFold 3风格的共折叠模型输出的物理有效性问题，同时保持结构准确性，无需重新训练，实现物理上有效的原子级结构预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法在预测生物分子复合物时存在物理误差，这限制了模型的应用。研究发现物理有效性可以仅通过模型采样过程中已有的量进行验证，因此提出了一个新的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 研究引入了两种局部、稀疏且位移限制的闭形式投影运算。一种是链间的范德华力投影，能推离最严重冲突的原子对；另一种是配体距离几何投影，恢复了键长、键角、内部接触、平面性以及手性。

Result: 这种方法在Boltz-2和OpenFold-3两个独立开发的模型上，在五个基准测试（CASP15、CASP16、PoseBusters单体和复合体集合，以及Boltz物理有效性测试集）中，恢复了完美的物理有效性，同时保持了结构准确性与配体定位指标，且几乎没有增加运行时间和内存开销。

Conclusion: 这种方法提供了一种实用且模型通用的方法，可以在不重新训练的情况下实现所有原子结构的物理有效性预测。

Abstract: AlphaFold 3-style cofolding models predict biomolecular complexes with high structural accuracy, yet a large fraction of their outputs are physically invalid: chains overlap at interfaces, ligand bond lengths and angles are distorted, rings are non-planar, and stereocentres are inverted. Current approaches either steer the sampler with physics-informed potentials, which multiplies sampling cost and memory overhead making inference impossible on large complexes, or finetune the model, costing time and tying the fix to one architecture. We observe that, unlike structural accuracy, physical validity is fully verifiable at inference time from quantities the sampler already holds. We therefore treat physical validity as a constrained inference problem and introduce two closed-form projection operators applied to the diffusion model's denoised clean-coordinate estimate, $\hat{x}_0$: an inter-chain van der Waals projection that pushes apart the most severely clashing atom pairs, and a ligand distance-geometry projection that restores bond lengths, angles, internal contacts, planarity and chirality. Both operators are local, sparse and displacement-capped, require no network evaluations, gradients or importance sampling, and leave the denoiser and its weights untouched, so they can be dropped into any AF3-style sampler without retraining.
