In agentic elicitation, these decisions may be delegated to a foundation model, yet how model choice shapes the resulting information-seeking behavior remains understudied. We study how judgments about information value vary across LLMs and how these differences shape sequential information seeking. We first examine these judgments across 11 LLMs spanning multiple model families and parameter scales, using a shared set of information and elicitation objectives. We then develop a controlled elicitation simulation in which different models encounter the same information space and use the same selection rule, isolating these judgments from question generation and respondent behavior. Using this setting, we characterize the breadth-depth behavior that emerges from model-specific information-seeking preferences over the course of elicitation. We further examine how interaction history changes the evaluation and subsequent selection of prospective information. We test the robustness and boundaries of these findings through sensitivity analyses and ablations over the opportunities available to the elicitor, the response labels used to operationalize information-seeking preferences, the presence of interaction history, and whether redundancy is explicitly relevant to the assessment. The project code, data, and trajectory files are available at https://github.com/infosenselab/open-elicitation.

</details>


### [178] [From Local Evidence to Safety Verdicts: Causal Tracing in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07514)
*Faezeh Dehghan Tarzjani,Mevan Wijewardena,Alexander Romanus,Sampad Mohanty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入SSU-Bench数据集，评估了三种视觉-语言模型在处理图像与提示以识别安全风险时的内部状态变化。研究发现，干预在变化输入位置上更早起作用，而干预在最终输入标记上则较晚起作用。通过对其他实例的估计方向也产生了类似的效果。最终标记状态的线性读出预测模型的判决，包括错误判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索视觉-语言模型在识别安全风险时内部状态的变化，以及这些变化的干预点。

Method: 通过构造一个包含安全和不安全图像-文本配对的数据集SSU-Bench，使用三种视觉-语言模型进行实验，转移输入配对的内部状态，并测量安全判断的变化。

Result: 研究发现，针对变化输入位置的干预在早期解码层有效，而针对最终输入标记的干预效果较晚出现。从其他示例估计的方向也产生类似的效果。最终标记状态的线性读出可以预测模型的决策，包括错误决策。

Conclusion: 研究确定了一种重复出现的过渡，即干预可以从哪里影响到联合安全判断，并区分了可读的模型决策与正确的安全判断。

Abstract: A vision-language model may need to combine an image with a prompt to recognize a safety risk that neither reveals alone. Where does this joint safety judgment become accessible inside the model? We introduce SSU-Bench, a dataset of matched safe and unsafe image-text combinations constructed using single-item prompt edits or image edits with annotated intended regions. Using three vision-language models, we transfer internal states between paired inputs and measure the resulting change in the safety verdict. Across models and both types of counterfactual, interventions at the changed input positions are effective in earlier decoder layers, while interventions at the final input token become effective later. Directions estimated from other examples produce similar late-layer effects. A linear readout of the final-token state also predicts the model's own verdict, including incorrect judgments, and cross-model comparisons reveal similarities in the patterns of counterfactual change. These findings identify a recurring transition in where interventions can influence a joint safety verdict and distinguish a readable model decision from a correct safety judgment.

</details>


### [179] [Grounding What Shapes the Plan: Rethinking Groundedness for Physical Intelligence in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07521)
*Minkyoung Cho,Zewei Zhou,Wenhao Ding,Shuhan Tan,Boyi Li,Yuxiao Chen,Yan Wang,Zheng Lian,Min-Hung Chen,Chaowei Xiao,Zhuoqing Mao,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Yulong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GroundAct 提出了一种新的规划方法，通过物理实体及其相互作用进行接地推理，确保规划过程中的每一步都基于具体的实体状态，从而实现从推理到执行的明确路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的驾驶模型虽然在因果关系、空间结构、感知证据和预测未来方面取得了进步，但如何通过具体的实体及其相互作用来保证最终行为的正确性是一个未解决的问题。

Method: GroundAct 使用一个轻量级的参考标记来保持选定实体的连续状态，仅通过这些参考实体及其与方案的互动来纠正计划，从而实现明确的路径规划。

Result: GroundAct 在开放环和闭环设置下都表现出了强大的规划能力，能够在正常、异常以及安全关键情况下进行有效规划，并且闭环实验也验证了其在仿真中的有效性能。

Conclusion: GroundAct 提出了一种新的规划方法，通过明确的路径从推理到行为，保证了实际驱动场景中的行为正确性以及系统的安全性。

Abstract: Driving models increasingly ground reasoning in causal relations, spatial structure, perceptual evidence, and predicted futures. These advances make reasoning more faithful to the driving scene, but leave a fundamental question unresolved: what should groundedness mean when the model ultimately outputs an action? Correctly grounded reasoning does not, by itself, ensure desirable driving outcomes. We introduce GroundAct, which starts from a simple premise: driving unfolds through physical entities and their interactions. Entities therefore become the unit of grounding; a lightweight reference token keeps each selected entity's continuous state addressable through symbolic reasoning; and only the referenced entities' interactions with the evolving proposal correct the plan. The result is an explicit path from what reasoning grounds to what the plan does, which we call grounded planning. To assess its practical value, we evaluate GroundAct in both open- and closed-loop settings. GroundAct shows strong open-loop planning across normal, out-of-distribution, and safety-critical scenarios, with closed-loop results extending this evidence to driving in simulation.

</details>


### [180] [A Systematic Investigation of Bias in Large Language Models for Advertising Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07544)
*Weiwei Wang,Yinchuan Xu,Jialu Gao,Youkow Homma,Jian Jiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统地探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在广告相关性判断中的公平性，通过反事实框架评估了广告商身份、语言、输入内容和人口统计学词汇的影响。研究发现，更改广告商身份或输入语言会影响相关性评估，且部分人口统计学关联显示出与刻板印象一致的模式。研究还考察了缓解措施的有效性，强调了依赖查询的相关性和训练数据中的广告商标签分布的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型被广泛用于评估广告与查询的相关性，其公平性问题尚未获得充分关注。因此，本研究旨在系统地评估这些模型在广告相关性判断中的公平性。

Method: 研究采用反事实框架，通过实际广告日志中的查询和广告对进行实验，并使用合成的受控查询研究人口统计学因素。研究了两种特定的LLM模型：GPT-4o和Qwen-7B，评估了广告商身份和语言变化对相关性评估的影响。此外，探究了缓解措施在模型推理和训练过程中的效果。

Result: 研究发现，更改广告商身份或输入语言可以改变相关性评估的结果。部分人口统计学因素（如性别和职业）显示出与刻板印象一致的模式。在缓解措施方面，其效果取决于查询相关性和训练数据中广告商标签的分布。

Conclusion: 研究结果有助于识别广告从业者中的公平风险，并为LLM相关性系统的缓解方法制定策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to judge how well an advertisement matches a query, but the fairness of these judgments has received limited attention. We conduct a systematic study of fairness in relevance judgments made by LLMs for queries and advertisements. Our counterfactual framework examines the effects of advertiser identity and possible popularity, input language, and demographic wording. We study GPT-4o as a categorical relevance judge and a Qwen-7B model trained specifically for relevance prediction. The advertiser and language experiments use query and advertisement pairs sampled from real advertising logs. Controlled synthetic queries are used to study demographic associations in employment, housing, and credit. For both models, changing the advertiser identity or input language can alter the relevance assessment. Selected demographic comparisons also show patterns consistent with common stereotypes, particularly those involving gender and occupation. We further study mitigation during model inference and training. The results indicate that its effectiveness depends on whether advertiser information is relevant to the query and how advertiser labels are distributed in the training data. These findings can help advertising practitioners identify fairness risks and develop suitable mitigation methods for LLM relevance systems.

</details>


### [181] [Decoupled Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07556)
*Xinle Wu,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeOrch通过分离任务分解和工作人员选择，实现更有效的多智能体系统构建，相比现有方法减少了工作人员的调用，并且在未见过的工人池中仍能保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动多智能体系统（MAS）编排方法将计划与固定的工人池耦合，并从相同的终端结果中学习分解和协作，这限制了迁移并模糊了责任分配。

Method: DeOrch采用两阶段规划方法，首先不依赖工人信息分解任务，接着利用工人身份无关的匹配反馈选择协作动作，匹配器使用固定探查集评估工人并在线调整。

Result: 在多种不同分布的任务中，DeOrch在较少的工人调用下超过先前的自动MAS编排方法，保持性能并在未见过的工人池中仍有效，两个部分都展现了持续的改进。

Conclusion: DeOrch通过分离任务规划和工人选择，成功提升了多智能体系统的性能，并展示了良好的迁移性和责任分配能力。

Abstract: Learned orchestration can automatically construct effective language-model multi-agent systems, but existing approaches couple planning to fixed worker pools and train decomposition and collaboration from the same terminal outcome, limiting transfer and obscuring credit assignment. We introduce DeOrch, which separates worker-agnostic planning from concrete worker selection. Its two-stage planner first decomposes the task without worker information, then chooses collaboration operations using compact, worker-identity-free matchability feedback from the pool, enabling conditional credit assignment to decomposition and collaboration decisions. A lightweight matcher estimates worker suitability from behavior on a fixed probe set and adapts online with a contextual bandit, allowing new workers to be incorporated without retraining the planner or matcher. Across diverse in- and out-of-distribution tasks, DeOrch outperforms prior automatic MAS orchestration methods with fewer worker calls than competing learned orchestrators, remains effective when transferred to an entirely unseen worker pool without retraining, and shows consistent gains from both components.

</details>


### [182] [Navigating Route Latent Space for Synthesizable Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07560)
*Tao Li,Tuan Vinh,Monika Raj,Yuan Fang,Zhichun Guo,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RouteFlow 提出了一个分子设计框架，通过连续的路径隐空间来优化合成分子，增强了目标属性并保证合成的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的合成导向分子设计方法要么返回与目标偏差较大的化合物，要么在合成空间中直接优化，缺乏有效的搜索环境。RouteFlow 的创新在于通过一个连续的路径隐空间来进行合成分子设计，使得合成可行性得以保持。

Method: RouteFlow 使用一个连续的路径隐空间，每个隐变量对应一个完整的合成路线。通过奖励导向的流匹配作为高效的采样器来指导搜索，同时引入循环一致性机制来稳定训练。

Result: 在 16 个优化任务中，RouteFlow 达到了最佳的样本效率，具有最优的合成可及性和最高的 retrosynthesis 成功率，同时证明了循环一致性机制的有效性。

Conclusion: RouteFlow 显示了通过连续路径隐空间进行合成导向分子设计的潜力，能够有效提升目标分子的合成可行性与性能。

Abstract: Goal-directed molecular design has advanced rapidly, yet a substantial proportion of designed molecules remain difficult to synthesize in practice, limiting their real-world utility. Prior synthesizability-aware methods either project generated molecules back to synthesizable analogs that deviate from the intended target, or optimize directly in discrete synthesis spaces that lack a continuous landscape for efficient search. We argue that this limitation mainly comes from the search space rather than the optimizer. To address this, we propose RouteFlow, a framework that reformulates synthesizable molecular design as a search over a continuous route latent space, where each latent maps back to a complete synthesis route and synthesizability is inherently preserved. To navigate this space, we adopt reward-guided flow matching as an efficient sampler that steers toward high-property regions. Since reward optimization may push latents off the manifold of real synthesis routes, where decoding becomes unreliable, we further introduce a cycle-consistency mechanism to stabilize fine-tuning. Across 16 optimization tasks from Therapeutic Data Commons, RouteFlow achieves the best sample efficiency among synthesizability-aware baselines, with the best synthetic accessibility and the highest retrosynthesis success rate. Our results also confirm that the proposed cycle-consistency reliably keeps optimization on-manifold while improving target properties, supporting effective synthesizable molecular discovery.

</details>


### [183] [Unanimously Wrong: Certified Abstention from How Medical LLM Consensus Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07570)
*Xiaoyang Wang,Tianrui Wang,Christopher C. Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ProbeGuard的认证弃权框架，该框架基于共识形成过程中的特征，通过过程信号来检测一致的决策结果是否正确，进而对具有高度一致性的决策结果进行风险评估，并给出了具体的应用及验证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于协议的医学问答系统中，一致性被误用为可靠性的衡量标准，但这种一致性并不总是反映正确性。为了提高医学问答系统的可靠性和准确性，研究者们提出了ProbeGuard框架。

Method: ProbeGuard框架从共识形成的特征中提取过程信号，包括一致性轨迹、少数派持续性及检索饱和度，对一致的决策结果进行细致分析。对于完全一致的情况，通过理由的语义熵检查一致性背后的原因是否一致，并进行主动探索获取反证以验证共识是否稳固。最后，通过分层学习和测试校准，为这些结果提供无分布依赖的风险界。

Result: 在三个医学问答基准测试和一个前沿参考上，ProbeGuard框架被应用于一个已发表的多轮代理自问答框架。结果显示，在MedQA上的统一表决中有13.4%是错误的，而没有基于协议的信号能够检测到它们。过程信号将正确共识和错误共识的辨别率提高到0.696 AUROC。认证规则能够解答六成的统一层次问题，且在域内校准数据累积后，准确率达到九成。

Conclusion: ProbeGuard框架成功地通过评估共识形成过程中的特征来辅助医学问答系统做出更可靠的决策。

Abstract: In clinical practice, agreement among independent experts is treated as evidence of reliability, and multi-round consensus has become a core mechanism of agentic medical question-answering systems. When such a system must decide whether to trust its own answer, the prevailing signal is again agreement, now among the sampled answers. But agreement is a fragile proxy for correctness. A system can be unanimously wrong, returning the same incorrect answer on every sample, and on these questions agreement-based signals carry no information. The cause is that these signals read only the final state of the consensus and discard how it was reached. Agreement that was reached by resolving disagreement with evidence looks identical, at the end, to agreement that was present from the first sample because every sample shares one misconception. ProbeGuard is a certified abstention framework that bases the abstention decision on how the consensus formed. Process features trace agreement trajectories, minority persistence, and retrieval saturation. For unanimous votes, rationale semantic entropy checks whether the reasons behind the vote cohere, and an active probe retrieves counter-evidence and measures whether the consensus survives. A stratified Learn-then-Test calibration then converts these scores into a distribution-free bound on selective risk. We evaluate ProbeGuard on three medical QA benchmarks and a hard-frontier reference, with a published multi-round agentic RAG substrate, against six abstention baselines. On MedQA, 13.4% of unanimous votes are wrong, and no agreement-based signal can flag them. Process signals raise the discrimination of correct from incorrect consensus from chance to 0.696 AUROC. The certified rule answers six in ten unanimous-layer questions at an observed selective risk of 9.0%, and nine in ten once in-domain calibration data accumulate.

</details>


### [184] [Cooperating with Future Collaborators: Multi-Agent RL under Staggered Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07578)
*Jianglin Qiao,Siyi Hu,Thien Hoang Nguyen,Zehong Cao,Salah Sukkarieh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为Staggered Participation Learning (SPL)的训练增强方法，在多层次的MARL框架、环境以及参与模式下评估了SPL的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了研究在任务中部分代理提前参与并留下有益信息的情境下的学习依赖性。

Method: 提出了一种名为SPL的训练增强方法，包括前向获取监督（Prospective Acquisition Supervision）和结果监督接收器学习（Outcome-Supervised Receiver Learning）。

Result: 在60种不同设置的比较中，SPL在所有情况下都实现了更高的任务完成率，并且在八个代理团队和基于物理的无人机-地面车辆环境（Isaac Lab）中也取得了显著的成效。

Conclusion: 研究证实了SPL方法在算法、时间和实体设置中的有效性和普遍性。

Abstract: In cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), agents are often trained under concurrent participation, while in many tasks some agents act earlier and leave task-relevant information that becomes useful to agents participating later. We study this setting as staggered participation (SP), which introduces a cross-time, cross-agent learning dependency because an early action may affect the return through the information it provides and the later policy that uses it. Learning under SP therefore requires both identifying what information is useful for future decisions and learning how later agents should use it. We propose Staggered Participation Learning (SPL), a training-time augmentation that addresses these two parts with prospective acquisition supervision for earlier agents and outcome-supervised receiver learning for later agents. We evaluate SPL across multiple policy-based MARL backbones, environments, and staggered-participation patterns. Across 60 MPE/RWARE backbone setting comparisons, SPL achieves higher observed mean task completion in every case, with an average difference of 14.1%. The gains also extend to eight-agent teams and a physics-based UAV-UGV environment in Isaac Lab, providing evidence across algorithmic, temporal, and embodied settings.

</details>


### [185] [LOGIC: An LLM Benchmark for Intent-Grounded Change Impact in Aerospace Electrical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07580)
*Muhammad Faraz Shoaib,Muhammad Qasim,Raisulhaq Mohammed Rizwan,Rahmatullah Safdar,Muzammil Adnan Shaik,Abdul Aleem Mohammed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍了一种名为LOGIC的基准和评估框架，该框架通过语言模型筛选候选变更，并通过类型化的电气可追溯图传播选定变更，从而区分候选筛选错误和下游传播错误。该研究评估了三种大型语言模型在特定场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在工程变更请求的执行过程中，可能导致过于广泛的影响报告，无法有效区分因候选选择错误和下游传播错误产生的差异，进而影响变更执行的精准度和效率。

Method: 开发了一个包含168个场景的控制基准框架LOGIC，其中包括144个选择场景和24个弃权场景。该框架使用本地可部署的语言模型根据请求生成一个确定性的候选变更库，并使用类型化的电气可追溯图来选择和传播变更。

Result: 通过对三种7-8B规模的语言模型进行评估，发现基于门的结构证据方法在选择过程中实现了完全准确的候选F1值（1.0000），而基于词的浅层匹配方法的F1值为0.9677；在关系同义词场景中基于词的匹配F1值仅为0.1772，使用模型接地的方法表现较为稳定；适度抑制错误的选择并保留合理的语义选择的空证据弃权策略在所有模型中的准确率提高了到0.6667，而异常报告率降低到了0.1667。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，结合语言模型的推理和工程审查，并在意图无法可靠确定时将这两种方法相结合，可以提高变更请求执行的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Aerospace electrical-design revisions can contain multiple genuine changes, although an engineering request may authorize only a subset. Propagating every detected difference can therefore produce overly broad impact reports. We present LOGIC, a controlled benchmark and evaluation framework in which locally deployable language models ground a request in a deterministic candidate-change inventory before selected changes are propagated through a typed electrical traceability graph. This separation permits candidate-selection errors to be distinguished from downstream propagation errors. LOGIC contains 168 scenarios, including 144 selection and 24 abstention cases. We evaluate three 7--8B models against intent-agnostic, lexical, and structured-evidence methods, with an oracle-root upper bound. On 96 explicitly anchored selection cases, gate-only structured evidence achieves candidate F1 of 1.0000, compared with 0.9677 for token-lexical matching. On 12 relational-paraphrase cases, token-lexical F1 is 0.1772 and gate-only F1 is 0.0000, compared with 0.5000--0.6400 for the large language models. Model grounding degrades as candidate inventories grow from 4 to 64 changes, while affected-element and typed-path accuracy remain comparatively stable when frozen selections are replayed over graphs of approximately 1K to 100K nodes. Strict evidence gating suppresses false positives but can remove correct semantic selections. An exploratory evidence-empty abstention policy raises strict abstention accuracy to 0.6667 for all three models and reduces unsafe-report rates to 0.1667, while decreasing answerable-case coverage by 16.0--27.1 percentage points. Four of six conflicting requests remain unsafe for each model. These findings support combining literal evidence and language-model reasoning with engineering review when intent cannot be established reliably.

</details>


### [186] [Representation Bias, Correction Transfer, and Resolution Sensitivity in Three-Dimensional Mitochondrial Morphometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07582)
*Farouk Ganiyu Adewumi,Timothy Oladunni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三维线粒体形态学的可靠性，通过不同实验发现体积测量存在系统偏差，并通过回归分析减少了误差，但指出准确性与不确定性的转移需要分别评估；同时，不同的解析度对线粒体表面面积和球度有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于量化成像管道中不同测量方法对同一对象产生的系统性差异，旨在评估和校正这些差异，以便更准确地研究线粒体的形态学变化。

Method: 使用3D Mitochondria Shape Library for Optical Microscopy数据集进行对照实验，首先通过边界分析识别偏差，然后控制性的重新实现标签管道并去除深度偏移，最后使用冻存回归分析减少误差。

Result: 结果显示，体积测量存在系统性偏差，深度偏移修正可减少约45%的误差，但部分误差尚未找到来源；冻存回归虽显著降低误差，但其总体准确性和低占用子群体的覆盖范围有限；不同的解析度下，线粒体表面面积和球度有明显变化。

Conclusion: 结论指出这一研究提供了量化检查，以区分处理诱导的描述符变化与潜在的生物学差异，而且首次具体研究了解析度对线粒体形态变化的影响。然而，仍需进一步研究以确认这些变化是否具有生物学意义。

Abstract: Quantitative imaging pipelines can produce precise but systematically different measurements of the same object. We present an empirical reliability assessment of three-dimensional mitochondrial morphometry that connects representation bias, a controlled processing intervention, correction transfer, and resolution sensitivity. Using 2,720 development objects from the 3D Mitochondria Shape Library for Optical Microscopy, we find that occupancy-derived volumes exceed reference mesh volumes by 3.665% on average despite an intraclass correlation coefficient of 0.994. Boundary analysis identifies an outward label displacement of 0.00304 normalized units. In a controlled label-pipeline reimplementation, removing the depth offset reduces volume error in all 55 analyzed objects by a mean of 1.57 percentage points, approximately 45% of mean reproduced inflation; the source of the remainder is not isolated. A frozen regression using occupancy-derived features reduces median absolute percentage error from 3.481% to 0.664% in 2,728 previously unused objects from the same resource. However, its calibrated error bound covers only 92.1% overall and 49.2% in a low-occupancy subgroup, demonstrating that accuracy and uncertainty transfer must be evaluated separately. In 550 rat-cortex objects from the MitoEM resource, coarsening in-plane spacing from 8 to 24 nanometers changes median surface area by minus 10.60% and sphericity by plus 11.76%, despite a rank correlation of 0.994. These results provide quantitative checks for distinguishing processing-induced descriptor changes from candidate biological differences, without establishing biological invariance or cross-source correction transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [ElecSafety: Diagnosing Large Language Model Safety Judgments for Microcontroller Boards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06905)
*Linjian Yang,Xinyan Wang,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 介绍了ElecSafety基准来评估LLM在电气安全性判断上的能力，通过提取制造商的约束和规则，并将场景分为多种类别，实验结果显示板规则能提高准确性，但对边缘情况和信息缺失情况效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要针对代码生成和硬件设计任务，而电气安全性判断缺乏针对特定板子的具体测试，故提出ElecSafety基准。

Method: 从供应商数据表和硬件手册中提取约束条件，场景设定包括决定性条件和黄金标签（安全、危险或无法确定），并翻译成自然语言。并测试了六个开源LLM的不同规则访问条件和标记限制。

Result: 板规则提高了准确性，但限制标记数影响较小。最优配置在边缘情况和信息缺失情况下仍然不可靠，正确标签往往基于不准确的约束。

Conclusion: 尽管板规则有助于提高准确性，但在处理边缘情况和缺乏信息的情景时仍存在问题，未来需要更全面地验证LLM的安全性判断能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) progressively support embedded hardware development, but a plausible recommendation may pose safety risks when acting on the physical hardware. Because electrical safety can be different between every microcontroller board, a practical question is raised: can LLMs have the capability to make the right decision on whether a proposed user operation is safe for the board? Although benchmarks for embedded development exist, they primarily target code generation and hardware design tasks. This highlights the lack of tested board-specific judgment regarding electrical safety. To address this gap, we introduce the ElecSafety benchmark to evaluate whether an LLM can respond with the correct label and derive that label from the proper manufacturer constraints and rules. We extract these constraints from vendor datasheets and hardware manuals. Each scenario is defined by a decisive condition and a gold label (safe, hazardous, or cannot determine) before translating the scenario into natural language. We categorize the scenarios into violation and compliant cases, board-swap, near-boundary, and missing-information. We then evaluated the scenario on six open-weight LLMs under various conditions of rule access and token limitations. The finding shows board rules can improve accuracy, whereas limiting the token has a smaller impact. Even the best configuration remains unreliable on near-boundary and missing-information scenarios, and a correct label often rests on the inaccurate constraints.

</details>


### [188] [FAPO: Fanout-Aware Post-Mapping Optimization for LUT-Based FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06937)
*Xiaoyu Hao,Keren Zhu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为FAPO的后映射优化器，它可以在不增加过多面积的情况下减少延迟，通过优化LUT裁剪和汇入分配来减少扇出瓶颈，并通过实验展示了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPGA技术映射后，逻辑深度减少不一定能消除LUT网络中的扇出瓶颈。传统的复制高负载LUT的方法虽然可以缓解瓶颈，但会增加面积并可能增加上游负载。本文旨在设计一种高效优化器FAPO，以减少扇出感知的面积-时间积（ATP）。

Method: FAPO通过计算最小切割，同时满足最终面积约束，联合精简汇入分配和LUT裁剪。该方法基于所有关键路径的必要条件，并结合网络的面积和时间进行优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，在EPFL基准测试上，使用三种初始映射器，FAPO将fanout-aware ATP减少了最多14.48%，并在5%的最终面积允许下，使得路由的关键路径延迟相对ABC降低了7.04%。

Conclusion: FAPO显著减少了FPGA设计中的扇出感知面积-时间积，并在实际测试中表现出色，为扇出优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Reducing logic depth during FPGA technology mapping does not necessarily eliminate fanout bottlenecks in the resulting LUT networks.Replicating heavily loaded LUTs can alleviate these bottlenecks, but the added copies consume area and may increase upstream loading. We propose FAPO, a mapper-independent post-mapping optimizer that jointly refines sink assignments and LUT cuts under a final-area constraint.Replication, cut switching, and recovery are evaluated by the resulting live LUT count.We derive a necessary all-critical-path condition for delay reduction and obtain minimum-copy proposals by minimum cut within a restricted same-cut family.These proposals complement greedy refinement, with mappings selected by full-network area and timing.Experiments on EPFL benchmarks with three initial mappers achieve geometric-mean reductions of up to 14.48% in fanout-aware area--timing product (ATP) with a 5% final-area allowance.VPR evaluation shows a 7.04% reduction in routed critical-path delay relative to ABC if.

</details>


### [189] [An Empirical Fault Vulnerability Exploration of ReRAM-based Process-in-Memory CNN Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07029)
*Aniseh Dorostkar,Hamed Farbeh,Hamid R. Zarandi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个故障注入框架，以研究基于ReRAM的PIM加速器在用于推断阶段的大规模CNN中的脆弱性。研究发现故障类型、层类型和深度，参数位置以及故障类型对CNNs在ReRAM-PIM加速器上的表现有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于ReRAM的PIM加速器在处理大型矩阵向量乘法时表现出色，但由于工艺变异和缺陷，这些加速器在安全性关键系统中的使用可能会因故障而受到阻碍。

Method: 开发了一个故障注入框架，通过将CNN学习参数映射到ReRAM交叉栏并在交叉阵列中注入故障，以评估不同故障模型（如粘连高和粘连低）对不同层和位置的影响。

Result: 研究显示，不同类型的故障对CNNs在ReRAM-PIM加速器上的分类性能有着显著影响。粘连低（SaL）比粘连高（SaH）进一步降低分类准确性。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调，软硬件层面的故障对基于ReRAM的PIM架构的大规模CNN的性能具有重大影响。

Abstract: Resistive random-access memory (ReRAM)-based Processing-in-Memory (PIM) accelerator is a promising platform for processing massively memory intensive matrix-vector multiplications of neural networks in parallel domain, due to its capability of analog computation, ultra-high density, near-zero leakage current, and non-volatility. Despite many advantages, ReRAM-based accelerators are highly error-prone due to limitations of technology fabrication that lead to process variations and defects. These limitations degrade the accuracy of Deep Convolutional Neural Networks (Deep CNNs) running on PIM accelerators. While these CNNs accelerators are widely deployed in safety-critical systems, their vulnerability to fault is not well explored. In this paper, we have developed a fault injection framework to investigate the vulnerability of large-scale CNNs at both software- and hardware-level of inference phases. Faulty ReRAM devices are another reliability challenges due to significant degradation of classification accuracy when CNN parameters are mapped to the accelerators. To investigate this challenge, we map the CNN learning parameter to the ReRAM crossbar and inject faults into crossbar arrays. The proposed framework analyzes the impact of stuck-at high (SaH) and stuck-at low (SaL) fault models on different layers and locations of CNN learning parameters. By performing extensive fault injections, we illustrate that the vulnerability behavior of ReRAM-based PIM accelerator for CNNs is greatly impressible to the types and depth of layers, the location of the learning parameter in every layer, and the value and types of faults. Our observations show that different models have different vulnerabilities to faults. Specifically, we show that SaL further reduces classification accuracy than SaH.

</details>


### [190] [SVRF: Efficient Register Storage for Long-Vector Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07078)
*Francesco Minervini,Lorenzo Deltetto,Osman Unsal,Adrian Cristal*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种标量化向量寄存器文件（SVRF），该设计将具备标量语义的向量值重定向到专用的标量寄存器文件，从而避免了不必要的全宽物理向量寄存器的分配和访问。该设计在RISC-V向量扩展（RVV）兼容的向量处理单元中实施并评估，研究显示该方法减少了向量寄存器压力和VRF活动，同时实现了最高15.8%的VRF面积减少和最高12%的总功耗减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的向量处理器虽然能提供极高的计算吞吐量，但由于非常长的向量可能导致向量寄存器文件（VRF）成为一个显著的面积、能量和寄存器压力来源。本文提出的是当向量指令执行具有标量语义的值时，这些值虽然从架构角度上以向量寄存器表示，但并不需要全宽度的存储。为了缓解这一问题，SVRF将这些标量语义的向量值重定向到专门的标量寄存器文件。

Method: 该设计通过在传统的向量处理器中引入SVRF，即将标量语义的向量值重定向到专用的标量寄存器文件，减少了不必要的全宽物理向量寄存器的分配和访问。该方法在RISC-V向量扩展（RVV）兼容的向量处理单元中实施并进行了评估。

Result: 在代表性HPC和AI/ML工作负载中，SVRF减少了向量寄存器压力和VRF活动，性能方面没有退步。该设计实现了最高15.8%的VRF面积减少和最高12%的总功耗降低。一些工作负载还表现出高达1.12倍的加速效果。

Conclusion: SVRF设计能够通过减少VRF需求来降低其面积和功耗，同时保持与支持的向量ISA兼容。在实际应用场景中，该设计可提供性能和能效的改进。

Abstract: Vector processors exploit data-level parallelism to provide high computational throughput, but very long vectors can make the Vector Register File (VRF) a significant source of area, energy, and register pressure. This cost is exacerbated when vector instructions operate on values with scalar semantics: although such values are architecturally represented using vector registers, they do not require full-width storage. This paper proposes the Scalarized Vector Register File (SVRF), a microarchitectural enhancement that redirects scalar-semantics vector values to a dedicated scalar register file, avoiding unnecessary allocation and access of full-width physical vector registers. The SVRF integrates with vector register renaming while preserving compatibility with the supported vector ISA. We implement and evaluate SVRF in an Register Transfer Level (RTL)-based RISC-V Vector Extension (RVV)-compliant vector processing unit supporting very long vectors. Across representative HPC and AI/ML workloads, the SVRF reduces vector-register pressure and VRF activity without degrading performance. By exploiting the resulting reduction in VRF demand, the proposed design achieves up to 15.8% VRF area reduction and up to 6% total area reduction, while reducing total power by up to 12% in evaluated configurations. Some workloads also achieve up to 1.12X speedup.

</details>


### [191] [Evaluating Inference Compute for Generative AI: A Framework for Enterprise Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07094)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui,Moona Zahid*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文分析了大规模语言模型（LLM）从单轮生成到有目的地执行多步骤任务的转变。这影响了推理硬件的经济性，特别是对于代理轨迹的需求，强调了保持权重在片上SRAM或编译器管理的层次存储中的重要性。论文还提出了评估框架和基准测试协议，旨在验证性能与成本效益之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文章目的是为了应对大规模语言模型从单次对话到计划、调用工具、读取结果并推理再行动的转变带来的挑战，尤其是对于经济学的影响和基础设施的优化。

Method: 通过采用 roofline 分析和封闭形式的事件延迟模型，论文展示了为什么在这一新阶段会优先选择特定类型的加速器，确定了每次步骤的可靠性随着任务长度呈指数增长，以及提出了一个多层评估框架。

Result: 研究表明，在长时间任务中，每步可靠性对性能的影响非常大，初步的评估框架和基准测试协议能够帮助验证模型的实际表现。

Conclusion: 论文最终提出了一种新的评估方法和基准测试协议，它不仅关注实际的性能表现，还考虑了成本效益问题，强调了在长时间任务中可靠性的重要性。

Abstract: LLM deployment is shifting from single-turn completion to agentic trajectories in which a model plans, calls tools, reads results and reasons at test time before acting. This inverts the economics of inference hardware: chat serving amortises weight reads across large batches, whereas agent trajectories are sequentially dependent, run at effective batch one, and make per-token decode latency (TPOT) the dominant term in task completion time. Using a roofline analysis and a closed-form episode-latency model, we show why this regime favours accelerators that keep weights in on-die SRAM (Cerebras WSE-3/3T, Groq/NVIDIA LPU) or compiler-managed tiered memory (SambaNova SN40L/SN50), and why three vendor ecosystems converged in 2026 on disaggregated prefill/decode serving. We show that per-step reliability compounds exponentially in trajectory length-a 2% per-step failure rate erases a 2x decode advantage for a 20-step agent-so determinism and tail latency are first-order performance variables. We then propose a four-layer evaluation framework (silicon, serving system, agent episode, enterprise) with a metric set built on goodput at an agentic SLO and cost per successful episode, a six-axis benchmark protocol over six task families, a paired-bootstrap statistical design, an attestation protocol for vendor-run benchmarks, and TCO, availability and adoption-timing models with explicit break-even conditions. All performance figures are public and labelled by evidence class; we state seven falsifiable hypotheses and the experiments that test them, and argue that the most likely original result is that token-throughput rankings diverge from cost-per-successful-task rankings on long-horizon work.

</details>


### [192] [Trail: Scalable and Low-Cost Temporal TLB Prefetching via Page-Table-Embedded Deltas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08483)
*Konstantinos Kanellopoulos,Konstantinos Sgouras,Harsh Songara,Rahul Bera,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Trail 是一种基于 TLB 的暂态预取器，它利用页面表条目中的非使用位存储地址差值，从而提高地址变换的效率。Trail 在 200 个翻译密集型工作负载中表现出色，平均提高了单核（四核）性能 5.7%（11.5%），超过之前的最好单独 TLB 预取器 1.7%（2.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 TLB 预取器在处理不规则访问时效果不佳，且固定的硬件容量无法与应用程序的内存足迹扩展。

Method: Trail 通过利用页面表条目中未使用的位来存储地址差值，并在产生页面表遍历时记录前一次发生遍历的区域与新区域之间的差值。

Result: Trail 在 200 个工作负载和 100 种多重程序中平均提高了单核（四核）性能 5.7%（11.5%），超过了最好的单独 TLB 预取器 1.7%（2.5%）。Trail 的硬件要求仅为 64 个条目的历史记录表。

Conclusion: Trail 提出了一种创新的 TLB 预取策略，通过拓展硬件容量并有效管理临时数据，显著提高了数据密集型工作负载的性能。

Abstract: Address translation is a major bottleneck in data-intensive workloads. TLB prefetching can hide translation latency, but existing spatial prefetchers struggle with irregular accesses, while temporal prefetchers store address deltas in fixed-capacity hardware that cannot scale with application memory footprints. Our characterization of 200 translation-intensive workloads reveals that each virtual memory region has a small, recurring set of deltas, and over 94% of deltas fit in 18 signed bits. We introduce Trail, a temporal TLB prefetcher that stores deltas in unused bits of leaf page table entries (PTEs). When a region triggers a page table walk, Trail identifies the region that last triggered a walk from the same instruction and records their delta in that source region's PTE. When a later walk fetches the source region's PTE cache block, Trail retrieves its deltas without additional memory accesses and prefetches translations for likely destination regions into the TLBs and cache hierarchy. Storing multiple deltas per PTE cache block improves coverage, while using existing PTE bits allows metadata capacity to scale with the application's memory footprint without additional metadata storage. Across 200 workloads and 100 multiprogrammed mixes, Trail improves single-core (four-core) performance by 5.7% (11.5%) on average over a baseline without TLB prefetching, outperforming the best prior standalone TLB prefetcher by 1.7% (2.5%). Trail requires only a 64-entry hardware table to track per-instruction page table walk history. Trail is freely available at https://github.com/CMU-SAFARI/Virtuoso/tree/trail-artifact-release.

</details>


### [193] [Agentic Design Space Exploration for Joint Hardware Configuration Selection and Mapping of AI Inference Workloads on Heterogeneous Edge SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07191)
*Geetha Prasuna Yarramneni,Surya Selvam,Wilfried Haensch,Anand Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TraceDSE是一种基于执行跟踪的代理设计空间探索流程，能够在异构SoC上对AI推理工作负载进行联合负载映射和处理器单元配置选择，相较于最先进的黑盒优化工具，TraceDSE在减少硬件评估次数的情况下，显著提升了效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代边缘SoC集成了多种性能和能效不同的处理单元，而AI推理工作负载在实时延迟和能效限制下的部署需要对这些处理单元进行联合映射和配置，传统的黑盒优化方法难以满足需求，LLM指导的设计空间探索也不够高效。

Method: TraceDSE通过一个迭代的提案者-批评者循环实现，利用来自系统的执行跟踪进行详细分析，以提出设计建议并通过LLM批评者分析来改进。

Result: 在Intel Meteor Lake SoC上对四个不同复杂性的AI推理模型及多模型流水线测试中，TraceDSE在帕累托前沿覆盖率上超越了NSGA-II和贝叶斯优化方法，分别提升了35%和68%，同时仅需约6-9倍较少的硬件评估。

Conclusion: TraceDSE改善了设计空间探索的效率和深度，为异构SoC的AI推理工作负载优化提供了新的视角和解决方案。

Abstract: Modern edge Systems-on-Chip (SoCs) integrate heterogeneous processing units (PUs) such as CPUs, GPUs, and NPUs, each with distinct performance and energy characteristics. Deploying AI inference workloads on them under real-time latency and energy constraints requires jointly mapping workloads to PUs and configuring each PU (e.g., selecting the number of active cores and the operating frequency). This joint space grows combinatorially, making exhaustive search infeasible. Most prior work on design space exploration (DSE) applies black-box optimization (BBO) such as evolutionary search, where each evaluation returns only aggregate metrics such as latency and energy. Recent LLM-guided DSE relies on the same sparse feedback. We observe that this limits its efficiency: it offers no insight into the design space or the reasons a design choice performs the way it does, and it leaves the reasoning abilities of LLMs largely unused. We present TraceDSE, an agentic DSE flow that performs joint workload mapping and PU configuration selection for AI inference on heterogeneous SoCs. TraceDSE is an iterative proposer-critic loop driven by richer feedback in the form of system execution traces. The LLM proposer agent generates candidate mappings and PU configurations for hardware evaluation. The LLM critic agent, equipped with programmatic trace-analysis tools, analyzes the traces to identify bottlenecks and suggest targeted refinements. This loop yields deeper insight into each design point, higher-quality decisions, and a more effective search. Across four AI inference workloads (models of varying complexity and a multi-model pipeline) on an Intel Meteor Lake SoC, TraceDSE consistently outperforms two state-of-the-art BBO tools, improving Pareto frontier hypervolume by up to 35% over NSGA-II and up to 68% over Bayesian optimization, while requiring ~6-9x fewer hardware evaluations.

</details>


### [194] [A Pipelined FPGA Architecture for Banded Sparse Matrix Dense Matrix Multiplication in Longformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07301)
*Phillip Pramberger,Athanasios Tziouvaras,Shreejith Shanker,George Floros*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种FPGA加速器，用于加速Longformer的带状SpMM。该设计利用了Longformer注意矩阵中可预测的稀疏模式，通过自定义按行存储方案和隐式索引，消除了传统稀疏矩阵格式的开销，同时允许定期的内存访问。加速器采用并行处理单元、流水线加法器树和双路径计算策略来最大化吞吐量和硬件利用率。该加速器在Vivado 2024.2中用Verilog实现，并在基于RFSoC的平台上进行评估，其钟周期超前延迟为11个周期时，仍维持在2.9瓦以下的功耗，并且以100 MHz的频率，达到了每秒1亿个点积结果，证明了直接利用结构化稀疏性进行稀疏transformer加速的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏transformer加速器主要针对注意力生成或非结构化稀疏性，而忽视了对于结构化稀疏注意力的稀疏矩阵-密集矩阵乘法（SpMM）加速。因此，本文的研究动机是填补这一空白，通过专门的FPGA加速器提高Longformer中带状SpMM的处理效率，以进一步优化长输入序列处理的transformer模型。

Method: 本文提出的设计通过自定义按行存储方案和隐式索引消除传统稀疏矩阵格式的开销，允许定期的内存访问。并行处理单元、流水线加法器树和双路径计算策略用于提高吞吐量和硬件利用率。该加速器通过在FPGA平台上使用Verilog编程，并在基于RFSoC的平台上进行评估。

Result: 在Vivado 2024.2中用Verilog实现的加速器，超前延迟11个时钟周期时，功耗低于2.9瓦，并以100 MHz频率实现了每秒超过10亿次点积结果。

Conclusion: 本文的工作有效地展示了如何直接利用结构化稀疏性来加速稀疏transformer中的SpMM，对于提高大型transformer模型的性能具有重要的推动作用。

Abstract: Sparse attention mechanisms have become increasingly important for transformer models processing long input sequences due to their lower computational and memory complexity compared to full self-attention. Longformer achieves this through a sliding-window attention mechanism that produces a structured banded sparse attention matrix. However, existing sparse transformer accelerators primarily target attention generation or unstructured sparsity, leaving sparse matrix--dense matrix multiplication (SpMM) for structured sparse attention largely unexplored. This paper presents a pipelined FPGA architecture for accelerating banded SpMM in Longformer. The proposed design exploits the predictable sparsity pattern of Longformer's attention matrix through a custom row-wise storage scheme with implicit indexing, eliminating the overhead of conventional sparse matrix formats while enabling regular memory accesses. The architecture employs parallel processing elements, pipelined adder trees, and a dual-path computation strategy to maximize throughput and hardware utilization. Implemented in Verilog and evaluated on an RFSoC platform using Vivado 2024.2, the accelerator sustains one complete dot-product result per clock cycle after an initial latency of 11 cycles while maintaining power consumption below 2.9 W. Operating at 100 MHz, the design achieves over 100 million dot-product outputs per second, demonstrating the effectiveness of directly exploiting structured sparsity for sparse transformer acceleration.

</details>


### [195] [A Shape-Adaptive Architecture with Disaggregated Quantization for Efficient LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07443)
*Cong Guo,Chiyue Wei,Bowen Duan,Haoxuan Shan,Benjamin F. Morris,Yintao He,Hai "Helen" Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: DynaCore 是一种统一架构，通过系统架构协同设计，旨在高效处理大型语言模型服务。该架构通过重塑计算单元和引入分阶段量化技术，显著降低了服务级延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型 (LLMs) 由于自回归生成方式和技术服务系统中的复杂性，其计算效率存在挑战。现有的加速器与系统水平行为不匹配导致了效能问题。

Method: DynaCore 通过重新设计计算单元和时间维度下的运算方式，利用体系结构与计算单元协同，且分阶段量化方式，优化了 LLM 服务过程中计算和通信的协调。

Result: 在实际服务跟踪下，DynaCore 显著降低了服务级别的延迟，相比量化和可重构加速器分别提升了 3.50x 和 2.97x 的 TTFT，36.55x 和 8.02x 的 TPOT。

Conclusion: DynaCore 提供了一种有效的解决方案，能够更好地适配 LLM 服务的复杂性，从而提升了处理速度和效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become the backbone of modern AI applications, but pose significant challenges for efficient inference. Their autoregressive generation divides execution into two phases: prefill, dominated by large GEMMs, and decoding, dominated by small GEMVs. Modern serving systems further introduce complexity through continuous batching and prefill-decoding disaggregation, leading to dynamic workloads and phase separation. However, existing accelerators remain poorly aligned with these system-level behaviors, resulting in inefficiencies in LLM serving.
  In this work, we present DynaCore, a unified architecture for efficient LLM serving via system-architecture co-design. We observe that the compute tile a systolic array executes, its Minimum Efficient Unit (MEU), spans all three GEMM dimensions. DynaCore reshapes the MEU along all three: spatially it trades array width against height asymmetrically, raising weight delivery while leaving the input path untouched, and temporally Split-K maps the reduction onto the array, folding partial sums through the interconnect the array already has. To exploit phase separation, we further propose disaggregated quantization, applying dual-side quantization to prefill and weight-only quantization to decoding, with an inner-product mixed-precision datapath that keeps output width invariant to precision. A runtime scheduling framework then selects an MEU per batch. Evaluation with real-world serving traces shows that DynaCore substantially reduces service-level latency over quantization and reconfigurable accelerators, improving TTFT by 3.50x and 2.97x and TPOT by 36.55x and 8.02x, respectively.

</details>


### [196] [Beyond No-Good Benders Cuts: Exact Realizability for Discretely Tunable Clock Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07600)
*Boxiang Song,Keren Zhu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究通过定义一个可验证的反馈接口解决了固定时钟树无法实现具有有用倾斜的时序约束的问题，通过算术证书和树特定的收缩方法，提出了新的实现性剪裁，能够在保持验证独立性的同时提高效率，但在某些情况下并非总是最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数字电路设计中，设计者需要确保时钟树的延迟满足特定的时序约束。然而，固定的时钟树无法通过离散的调谐选择实现具有有用倾斜的所有调度方案。研究动机在于开发一种可以验证的反馈接口，确保所需调度方案的实现性同时提高验证效率。

Method: 研究通过将无法实现的调度目标表示为相对于固定时钟树的具体延迟关系的标准成员，开发了一种可验证的反馈接口。使用算术证书能够解释无法实现的目标，而特定树的收缩则减少了投影到选定终端的结构性关系，这些关系成为实现性剪裁。

Result: 研究提出的方法相比单一的无用剪裁能够排除更多候选方案，包括线性不平等无法分离的离散孔。实验表明，该方法能减少或acles调用次数和加快投影构建，但也揭示了一些重要限制。

Conclusion: 研究的贡献在于提供了一种独立验证的调度树接口，这是为了提高实现性验证的效率而非声称加速通用求解器或物理最终验证。

Abstract: Useful-skew schedules can satisfy timing constraints yet remain unrealizable by a fixed clock tree with discrete tuning choices. We formulate this mismatch as exact membership in a finite relative-latency set and develop a checkable feedback interface between the scheduler and the tree model. Arithmetic certificates explain unrealizable targets, while tree-specific contraction reduces the structural size of the exact relations projected onto selected sinks. These relations become realizability cuts that can exclude more candidates than a no-good on the same certificate support, including discrete holes that linear inequalities cannot separate. Controlled experiments confirm fewer oracle calls and faster projection construction. They also expose important limits: globally minimum certificates can cost more than they save, compact arithmetic feedback can fail on non-parity obstructions, and direct monolithic optimization remains faster on the tested additive models. The contribution is an exact, independently verifiable scheduler--tree interface, rather than a claim of universal solver acceleration or physical signoff.

</details>


### [197] [CACHEFORGE: LLM-Guided End-to-End Generative Cache Replacement Policy for Performance and Hardware Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07668)
*Kaushal Mhapsekar,Bita Aslrousta,Brijesh Kumar Bhayana,Paula Contreras,Azam Ghanbari,Ethan Goodman,Anna Andriiko,Samira Mirbagher Ajorpaz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CACHEFORGE 是一个通过结合大型语言模型和硬件感知循环来进化缓存替换策略的框架，能够提高缓存命中率和指令每周期数，特别在内存密集型任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缓存替换策略主要依赖于手工设计、启发式特征工程的预测器或是无法自动生成新决策逻辑的离线模仿模型，这导致性能提升有限且容易过拟合。

Method: CACHEFORGE 使用大型语言模型 (LLM) 在一个由反馈循环组成的架构中提出新的 C++ 替换逻辑，并通过 CRC-2 ChampSim 模拟器评估策略的有效性。通过奖励塑形、结构检查、动态突变、温度调度和跨策略交叉等多种方法，确保所生成的策略满足硬件限制并探索超出固定预测器结构的算法变换。

Result: CACHEFORGE 在 SPEC CPU2006 测试集上的表现优于所有 CRC-2 基线，总命中率提高了 27.36% 至 11.83%，指令每周期数提高了 10.15% 至 2.71%。

Conclusion: CACHEFORGE 通过结合大型语言模型和硬件感知循环，能够发现并实现满足硬件约束的嵌套策略与算法变换，从而显著提升缓存性能。

Abstract: Modern cache replacement designs saturate because they operate within fixed representational structures, hand-crafted and heuristic based feature-engineered predictors, or offline imitation models that cannot generate new decision logic on their own. At the same time, replacement is shaped by the causal interaction of prefetching, thrashing, spatial locality, and access-type behavior, producing an enormous design space that is difficult to traverse manually. Prior approaches typically rely on heuristics, parameter tuning, or imitation of an offline optimal policy, capturing correlations rather than synthesizing new mechanisms. As a result, their performance gains often plateau and they overfit under dynamic workload conditions.
  CACHEFORGE is the first framework to evolve cache-replacement policies end-to-end by embedding a large language model inside a governed hardware-aware loop. In each iteration, the LLM proposes new C++ replacement logic, the policy is evaluated under a trace-based CRC-2 ChampSim simulator, and the framework enforces feasibility through reward shaping, structural checks, dynamic mutation, temperature scheduling, and cross-policy crossover. This closed-loop generation-evolution loop specifically designed for cache replacement policy enables the discovery of compact policies that satisfy hardware constraints while exploring algorithmic transformations beyond fixed predictor structures.
  Across SPEC CPU2006, CACHEFORGE outperforms all CRC-2 baselines. It improves the total hit rate by 27.36%, 19.69%, 13.72%, 13.15%, 11.83%, and 5.73% over MPPPB, ReD, Hawk-eye, SHiP++, LIME, and LRU, respectively. On memory-intensive workloads, it increases IPC by 10.15%, 7.89%, 6.34%, 3.64%, 3.12%, and 2.71% over LRU, MPPPB, LIME, ReD, SHiP++, and Hawkeye.

</details>


### [198] [CHiRP: Control-Flow History Reuse Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07685)
*Samira Mirbagher-Ajorpaz,Elba Garza,Gilles Pokam,Daniel A. Jiménez*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种专为 TLB 设计的预测替换策略 CHiRP，并表明该策略相较于其他策略能显著降低 TLB 未命中率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中很少关注 TLB 管理中的替换策略，而有效的替换策略对于提高 TLB 性能至关重要。

Method: 研究首先将近期提出的预测缓存替换策略应用于 TLB，然后引入了一种针对 TLB 优化的预测策略——Control-flow History Reuse Prediction (CHiRP)。

Result: 在 1024 项 8 路集的 L2 TLB 和 4KB 页大小下，CHiRP 策略平均降低了 28.21% 的每千条指令 TLB 未命中次数 (MPKI)，优于其他现有策略。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了如何通过针对 TLB 特有的历史行为设计合适的预测策略，来有效降低 TLB 未命中率。

Abstract: Translation Lookaside Buffers (TLBs) play a critical role in hardware-supported memory virtualization. To speed up address translation and reduce costly page table walks, TLBs cache a small number of recently-used virtual-to-physical address translations. TLBs must make the best use of their limited capacities. Thus, TLB entries with low potential for reuse should be replaced by more useful entries. This paper contributes to an aspect of TLB management that has received little attention in the literature: replacement policy. We show how predictive replacement policies can be tailored toward TLBs to reduce miss rates and improve overall performance. We begin by applying recently proposed predictive cache replacement policies to the TLB. We show these policies do not work well without considering specific TLB behavior. Next, we introduce a novel TLB-focused predictive policy, Control-flow History Reuse Prediction (CHiRP). This policy uses a history signature and replacement algorithm that correlates to known TLB behavior, outperforming other policies. For a 1024-entry 8-way set-associative L2 TLB with a 4KB page size, we show that CHiRP reduces misses per 1000 instructions (MPKI) by an average 28.21% over the least-recently-used (LRU) policy, outperforming Static Re-reference Interval Prediction (SRRIP), Global History Reuse Policy (GHRP) and SHiP, which reduce MPKI by an average of 10.36%, 9.03% and 0.88%, respectively.

</details>


### [199] [A gem5-based Simulation Framework for Computing-in-DRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08186)
*Alexander Kusnezoff,João Paulo C. de Lima,Jeronimo Castrillon,Asif Ali Khan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了gem5-CIMD，一个基于最新gem5版本的全应用CIMD模拟框架，扩展了指令集并增强了内存管理功能，能够准确模拟CIMD工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试环境（贵）且耗时，开源模拟器主要依赖跟踪且无法执行完整应用运行。此外，目前唯一的全应用CIMD模拟器基于过时的gem5版本。

Method: 扩展了gem5的模拟功能，包括增加16种CIM指令，优化内存管理以支持CIM操作，并准确模拟DRAM刷新操作。

Result: gem5-CIMD可以完整模拟和调度CIMD工作负载，包括端到端的KNN工作负载，并提供了一个CIM标准库作为编译目标。

Conclusion: gem5-CIMD为研究人员和开发者提供了一个强大的CIMD仿真平台，以研究和优化CIMD架构。

Abstract: Computing-in-Memory using DRAM (CIMD) has demonstrated substantial energy and throughput gains for memory-bound workloads consisting of bulk-bitwise operations, by performing computation directly within DRAM subarrays. Realizing CIMD, however, requires a redesign of the memory controller and careful mapping of operands onto the memory arrays. Presently, accurate FPGA-based testbeds exist, but they are costly and labor-intensive, while open-source simulators are largely trace-based and cannot execute full application runs. The only full-application CIMD simulator available, included with MIMDRAM, is built on an outdated version of gem5 and its toolchains. We present gem5-CIMD, a full-application CIMD simulation framework built on the latest version of gem5. We extend the simulated instruction set from four bitwise operations to sixteen CIM instructions spanning arithmetic, relational, conditional, and utility operations across a range of data types and bitwidths (4 to 64 bits). A complete memory-management stack comprising multi-level huge-page support, a CIM-aware allocator, and a subarray-aware address-mapping scheme ensures that CIM operands are co-located in the same DRAM subarray. In addition, gem5-CIMD is aware of the DRAM refresh interval and faithfully models and schedules the mandatory refresh operations when needed. We provide a CIM standard library intended as a compiler target and evaluate gem5-CIMD on multiple case studies including an end-to-end KNN workload. The simulator sources, including the evaluated workloads, are publicly available.

</details>


### [200] [On the Impact of Degradation-Balanced Scheduling in Manycore Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08190)
*Maria Pantazi-Kypraiou,George Goudroumanis,George Floros,Anuj Pathania,George Stamoulis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究分析了多核心系统中电迁移退化与任务分配之间的关系，并提出了一种称为Reliability-Balanced (RB)的调度策略，以平衡各核心间的电迁移风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多核心系统中，任务在核心间的分配会影响功率、温度和电流密度，进而影响电迁移退化的空间分布。传统的基于当前可靠性的调度策略在可靠性值接近时可能无法区分候选核心，导致调度决策受次要因素驱动，而非长期平衡退化。

Method: 研究提出了一个新的调度策略——Reliability-Balanced (RB)策略，通过预测每种任务分配的全任务影响，对该策略进行了实验验证。

Result: 在随机的连续混合工作负载下，所提出的调度器保持了已完成的任务数量，同时平均R值提高了0.37%，最小R值提高了4.35%。此外，R值、暴露、应力和活跃时间的变异系数分别减少了10.84%、9.39%、8.35%和16.38%。

Conclusion: Reliability-Balanced 策略在提高系统可靠性的同时，减少了资源分布的不均衡，是一个有效的电迁移平衡调度策略。

Abstract: Electromigration degradation in manycore systems depends strongly on how runtime workload is distributed across cores. Task placement affects power, temperature, and current density, which in turn shape the spatial distribution of degradation over time. However, a scheduler that ranks cores only by their current reliability state may not always distinguish among candidate cores, especially when reliability values are close at decision time. In this case, scheduling decisions can be driven by secondary factors rather than by long-term degradation balance. This paper studies degradation-balanced scheduling and proposes a Reliability-Balanced (RB) policy that predicts the full-task effect of each candidate assignment. RB penalizes added imbalance in electromigration exposure, stress, active time, and task count while guarding the predicted Rvalue floor. On randomized continuous mixed workloads, the proposed scheduler preserves completed tasks while improving average Rvalue by 0.37% and minimum Rvalue by 4.35%. It reduces Rvalue, exposure, stress, and active-time standard deviation by 10.84%, 9.39%, 8.35%, and 16.38%, respectively.

</details>


### [201] [ECO: Energy-Oriented Configuration Optimization for Attention FFN Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08373)
*Zou Qingyun,Bin Gao,Zhuobin Huang,Weng-Fai Wong,Tulika Mitra,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ECO算法通过联合搜索部署结构及其可接受的操作控制，以在有限的测量预算下优化低能耗配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在确保延迟和吞吐量服务水平目标（SLOs）的情况下，减少LLM服务的GPU能耗。

Method: ECO算法结合结构感知的能量先验、基于高斯过程的学习残差预测误差以及成本意识的条件贝叶斯优化，优先选择预期能耗改进最大的测量。

Result: ECO减少了所有16个场景中的服务能耗40.5%，并在平均输出令牌速率上提高了20.7%，而在基线模型上实现目标SLOs。与典型条件贝叶斯优化相比，8个A6000场景中选择的可行能耗平均低33.1%，与遗传搜索相比低25.8%。

Conclusion: ECO算法能够在有限测量预算下有效寻找低能耗配置，同时保障服务性能。

Abstract: Energy-efficient LLM serving requires minimizing serving GPU energy while meeting latency and throughput service-level objectives (SLOs). Attention--FFN disaggregation (AFD) enables separate resource allocation and operating controls for attention and expert computation, but their energy effects remain coupled through the execution pipeline. Realizing its energy-saving potential therefore requires navigating a hierarchical configuration space in which deployment structures constrain admissible controls and shape their end-to-end effects. Finding low-energy configurations that meet SLOs is challenging because physical evaluations are costly and only a small fraction of candidates can be measured. We present Energy-Oriented Configuration Optimization (ECO), which jointly searches deployment structures and their admissible operating controls under a limited measurement budget. ECO constructs a structure-aware energy prior from calibrated stage behavior and pipeline dependencies, then learns residual prediction errors with a Gaussian process. Its cost-aware constrained Bayesian optimization prioritizes measurements according to expected energy improvement while accounting for SLO feasibility, execution success, and evaluation cost, and returns the lowest-energy measured feasible configuration. Across all 16 scenarios on A6000 and A100 with Qwen and DeepSeek, ECO's frozen configurations, evaluated on disjoint requests, reduce serving energy by 40.5\% and increase output token rate by 20.7\% on average relative to baselines while meeting target SLOs. Across the 8 A6000 scenarios, its selected feasible energy averages 33.1\% below generic constrained Bayesian optimization and 25.8\% below genetic search.

</details>


### [202] [Lachesis: Lifetime-Aware KV Cache Placement for Agent Serving across HBM and High-Bandwidth Flash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08378)
*Jaehoon Yang,Jeongmin Lee,Haneul Park,Seung Yul Lee,Nam Sung Kim,Jae W. Lee*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种在高带宽闪存(HBF)和高带宽内存(HBM)之间基于键值缓存生命周期进行智能分配的方法，提升了系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前，大型语言模型（LLM）服务主要采用代理工作负载模式，代理和其子代理在多次请求中积累上下文，消耗大量内存。为解决这一问题，研究提出Lachesis方案，旨在优化键值缓存的放置。

Method: 该研究基于代理运行的生命周期来决定数据应放置于HBM还是HBF，引入了Lachesis层与代理编排程序及服务引擎之间进行交互，并根据键值缓存的生命周期在写入时决定放置位置，阅读后则释放存储。

Result: 实验结果表明，Lachesis能够将HBF寿命延长1.19-3.13倍，达到3.3-12.2设备年。即使在长时间满负荷运行下，HBF也能超过其五年的保修期。

Conclusion: Lachesis通过智能分配键值缓存位置，有效延长了缓存系统在针对多代理场景中的使用寿命。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving is increasingly dominated by agentic workloads, in which agents and their sub-agents accumulate context as KV cache across many requests, consuming substantial memory. High-bandwidth flash (HBF) is a promising solution, providing an order of magnitude greater capacity at HBM-class read bandwidth, but its finite write endurance is the key limiting factor. Our key insight is that KV cache should be placed across HBM and HBF by its lifetime. Placing shorter-lived data in HBM lets HBM absorb more of an agent run's writes and sends less of them to HBF. As the lifetime of KV cache in agentic serving is dictated by the harness, the program that orchestrates the agents, we analyze its behavior and identify three axes along which lifetime diverges, temporal, structural, and inter-worker. Guided by these observations, we present Lachesis, a lifetime-aware KV cache placement layer between the agent harness and the serving engine. At write time, it places each segment in HBM or HBF according to its lifetime, and frees its blocks once the segment is no longer read. In trace-driven simulation, Lachesis extends HBF lifetime by 1.19-3.13x over HBM-first placement, reaching 3.3-12.2 device-years. Even under continuous 24x7 operation at the full load a tight SLO admits, HBF outlasts its five-year warranty on the multi-agent trace.

</details>


### [203] [X-OPM: Explainable Automatic Digital On-Chip Power Modeling for Enhanced Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08502)
*Jingbo Jiang,Xizi Chen,Jian Peng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: X-OPM 通过对特征进行工程化处理并结合树模型和线性模型，提供了高效的电源管理解决方案，其在C906向量处理器上表现出色，优于其他方法且面积开销小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的电源管理方法主要采用端到端的训练方式，未充分考虑特征的物理可解释性，导致泛化能力不足。X-OPM 通过引入树模型捕获特征交互，结合线性模型进行预测，旨在提高电源管理系统的预测精度。

Method: X-OPM 使用树模型捕捉特征之间的交互，并利用线性模型进行预测。同时，它包括一个以人为本的流程，平衡模型准确性和建模工作量。

Result: X-OPM 在C906处理器上实现了 $R^2 > 0.93$ 的预测效果，并且其面积开销小于1%，优于APOLLO、COBIT和标准MLP等现有方法。

Conclusion: X-OPM 提出了一个特征工程框架，通过树模型与线性模型结合，能够提供高效的电源管理解决方案，解决了传统方法在特征物理解释性和泛化能力上的问题。

Abstract: Proactive power management systems reduce processor dynamic power through runtime power prediction and power-aware scheduling. Accurate, stable and low-overhead digital on-chip power meters (OPMs) are crucial for improving the prediction quality. Recent studies have explored various modeling methods, including using linear models, decision trees, and multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) to construct OPMs. However, most current approaches train models end-to-end without analyzing the physical interpretability of features, affecting their ability to generalize to unseen workloads. Grounded in the design principles of synchronous digital VLSI circuits, X-OPM introduces a robust feature engineering framework that uses tree-based models to capture feature interactions and linear models for prediction. It also incorporates a human-in-the-loop workflow to balance model accuracy against modeling effort. Evaluated on a commercial C906 vector processor, X-OPM consistently achieves $R^2 > 0.93$ across all workloads with sampling window size set below $8$ cycles. In contrast, state-of-the-art methods including APOLLO, COBIT, and standard MLPs fail to generalize across all test cases. Layout with commercial EDA tools shows that X-OPM incurs an area overhead below $0.1\%$, which is on par with lightweight tree-based and linear models, and significantly smaller than MLP-based models.

</details>


### [204] [A Framework for Accelerating Transformer Inference on RISC-V for Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08688)
*Ajay Kumar M,Vishnu PS,Yike Li,Shreejith Shanker,Dimitrios S. Nikolopoulos,Bo Ji,Hans Vandierendonck,Deepu John*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种针对资源受限的IoT设备加速基于Transformer的语言模型的框架，通过定制指令集扩展和编译器引导的循环展开策略，显著提高推理速度，同时控制硬件成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对嵌入式设备上运行的紧凑型语言模型，该研究旨在开发一种高效的加速框架，以提高推理性能，同时保持低功耗和小面积。

Method: 研究通过设计轻量级指令集扩展，特别针对这些模型的非显式计算模式；引入了加速批量矩阵乘法地址生成阶段的自定义指令；应用了可选的编译器引导的循环展开策略，通过增加代码大小来减少总体执行时间。

Result: 在Synopsys trv32p3f RISC-V核心上实现了最高2.19倍的推理加速；在FPGA实现上，AMD Zynq UltraScale+ ZCU102的芯片面积开销为32.94%；使用TSMC 28 nm库的ASIC实现，面积开销为21.08%，时钟频率为250 MHz。

Conclusion: 该框架在嵌入式设备上提供了显著的推理速度提升，同时控制了芯片面积和功耗，展示了在实际硬件平台上的可实施性。

Abstract: This work presents a framework for accelerating transformer-based language models (LMs) on resource-constrained IoT devices. The framework targets compact LMs: BERT-Tiny (B-Ty), MobileBERT (M-Bt), MiniLM (M-Lm), Electra (E-Lt) and DeBERTa (D-Bt) -- selected for their architectural diversity and use in edge inference scenarios. The proposed flow derives lightweight instruction set extensions tailored to the non-obvious computational patterns of these models. In addition, a custom instruction is introduced to accelerate the address generation stage of batch matrix multiplication, achieving a performance improvement of 15.39--21.74% with modest ASIC overheads of 6.79% in area and 2.33% in power. To further enhance performance without incurring additional processor core hardware cost, an optional compiler-directed loop unrolling strategy is employed, trading increased code size for overall reduced execution time. Evaluation on the Synopsys trv32p3f RISC-V core demonstrates inference speedups of up to 2.19x, and FPGA implementation on the AMD Zynq UltraScale+ ZCU102 shows a 32.94% area overhead at 75 MHz, whereas the ASIC implementation using the TSMC 28 nm library incurs a 21.08% area overhead while operating at 250 MHz.

</details>
